import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy import func, select, text, update
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.models.devices import DeviceCalibration, DeviceModel
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, QualityStatus, Role
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.tenancy import AuditLog, Tenant, User
from app.schemas.schemas import (AdminMeasurementOut, AdminUserOut, AdminUserUpdate, AlgorithmStateOut, AuditLogOut,
                               CalibrationCreate, CalibrationOut, CellOut, DeviceModelCreate, DeviceModelOut,
                               OverrideRequest, QualitySummaryOut, SettingsOverrides)
from app.schemas.common import Msg, Page
from app.models.collection import TenantAlgorithmState
from app.services import collection as collection_service
from app.services.audit import audit
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

_admin = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin)
router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("admin", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])


# ----- utilisateurs
@router.get("/users", response_model=Page[AdminUserOut])
async def users(role: Role | None = None, is_active: bool | None = None, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
                offset: int = Query(0, ge=0), admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    conds = [User.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id]
    if role:
        conds.append(User.role == role)
    if is_active is not None:
        conds.append(User.is_active == is_active)
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(*conds))).scalar_one()
    rows = (await db.execute(select(User).where(*conds).order_by(User.created_at.desc())
                             .limit(limit).offset(offset))).scalars().all()
    return Page(items=rows, total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset)


@router.patch("/users/{user_id}", response_model=AdminUserOut)
async def update_user(user_id: uuid.UUID, payload: AdminUserUpdate, request: Request,
                      admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    target = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if target is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "user_not_found")
    if target.id == admin.id:
        raise HTTPException(409, "cannot_modify_self")
    changes = payload.model_dump(exclude_none=True)
    for k, v in changes.items():
        setattr(target, k, v)
    await audit(db, admin, "user.update", "user", target.id, {k: str(v) for k, v in changes.items()}, request)
    await db.commit()
    return target


# ----- mesures (vue anonymisée : jamais de coordonnées ni d'identité)
@router.get("/measurements", response_model=Page[AdminMeasurementOut])
async def measurements(status: QualityStatus | None = None, h3_index: str | None = None,
                       limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200), offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
                       admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    conds = [Measurement.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id]
    if status:
        conds.append(Measurement.quality_status == status)
    if h3_index:
        conds.append(Measurement.h3_index == h3_index)
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(Measurement).where(*conds))).scalar_one()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement, MeasurementQuality)
        .outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == Measurement.id)
        .where(*conds).order_by(Measurement.measured_at.desc()).limit(limit).offset(offset))).all()
    items = [AdminMeasurementOut(
        id=m.id, measured_at=m.measured_at, h3_index=m.h3_index, day_part=m.day_part,
        laeq_db=round(m.laeq + m.correction_db_applied, 1), lmax_db=round(m.lmax + m.correction_db_applied, 1),
        calibration_status=m.calibration_status, quality_status=m.quality_status,
        quality_score=q.score if q else None, reason_codes=[r["code"] for r in (q.reasons if q else [])],
        evaluator=q.evaluator if q else None, contributor_token=m.contributor_hash[:12],
        noise_context=m.noise_context) for m, q in rows]
    return Page(items=items, total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset)


@router.post("/measurements/{measurement_id}/override", response_model=Msg)
async def override(measurement_id: uuid.UUID, payload: OverrideRequest, request: Request,
                   admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    m = await db.get(Measurement, measurement_id, with_for_update=True)
    if m is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "measurement_not_found")
    status = QualityStatus(payload.status)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    q = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == m.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if q is None:
        q = MeasurementQuality(tenant_id=m.tenant_id, measurement_id=m.id, status=status, score=0.0,
                               sub_scores={}, reasons=[], evaluator="admin", evaluated_at=now)
        db.add(q)
    q.status, q.evaluator, q.override_by, q.evaluated_at = status, "admin", admin.id, now
    q.reasons = [*(q.reasons or []), {"code": "admin_override", "severity": "info", "reason": payload.reason}]
    m.quality_status, m.aggregated_at = status, None
    await audit(db, admin, "measurement.override", "measurement", m.id, {"status": payload.status}, request)
    await db.commit()
    return Msg(detail="overridden")


# ----- appareils et calibration
@router.get("/device-models", response_model=list[DeviceModelOut])
async def device_models(admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return (await db.execute(select(DeviceModel).where(DeviceModel.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id)
                             .order_by(DeviceModel.manufacturer, DeviceModel.model))).scalars().all()


@router.post("/device-models", response_model=DeviceModelOut, status_code=201)
async def create_device_model(payload: DeviceModelCreate, request: Request,
                              admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    dm = DeviceModel(tenant_id=admin.tenant_id, **payload.model_dump())
    db.add(dm)
    try:
        await audit(db, admin, "device_model.create", "device_model", dm.id, request=request)
        await db.commit()
    except IntegrityError:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(409, "device_model_exists")
    return dm


@router.get("/device-models/{model_id}/calibrations", response_model=list[CalibrationOut])
async def calibrations(model_id: uuid.UUID, admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return (await db.execute(select(DeviceCalibration).where(DeviceCalibration.device_model_id == model_id)
                             .order_by(DeviceCalibration.created_at.desc()))).scalars().all()


@router.post("/device-models/{model_id}/calibrations", response_model=CalibrationOut, status_code=201)
async def add_calibration(model_id: uuid.UUID, payload: CalibrationCreate, request: Request,
                          admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    dm = await db.get(DeviceModel, model_id, with_for_update=True)
    if dm is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "device_model_not_found")
    await db.execute(update(DeviceCalibration).where(DeviceCalibration.device_model_id == model_id)
                     .values(is_active=False))
    cal = DeviceCalibration(tenant_id=admin.tenant_id, device_model_id=model_id, calibrated_by=admin.id,
                            is_active=True, **payload.model_dump())
    db.add(cal)
    dm.calibration_status, dm.correction_db = CalibrationStatus.calibrated, payload.correction_db
    await audit(db, admin, "device_model.calibrate", "device_model", model_id,
                {"correction_db": payload.correction_db}, request)
    await db.commit()
    celery_app.send_task("measurements.reevaluate_model", args=[str(admin.tenant_id), str(model_id)])
    return cal


@router.post("/device-models/{model_id}/uncalibrate", response_model=Msg)
async def uncalibrate(model_id: uuid.UUID, request: Request, admin: User = Depends(_admin),
                      db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    dm = await db.get(DeviceModel, model_id, with_for_update=True)
    if dm is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "device_model_not_found")
    await db.execute(update(DeviceCalibration).where(DeviceCalibration.device_model_id == model_id)
                     .values(is_active=False))
    dm.calibration_status, dm.correction_db = CalibrationStatus.uncalibrated, None
    await audit(db, admin, "device_model.uncalibrate", "device_model", model_id, request=request)
    await db.commit()
    celery_app.send_task("measurements.reevaluate_model", args=[str(admin.tenant_id), str(model_id)])
    return Msg(detail="uncalibrated")


# ----- zones
@router.get("/cells", response_model=Page[CellOut])
async def cells(limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500), offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
                admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(GeographicCell).where(
        GeographicCell.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id))).scalar_one()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(GeographicCell.h3_index, func.ST_Y(GeographicCell.center), func.ST_X(GeographicCell.center),
               GeographicCell.last_measurement_at).where(GeographicCell.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id)
        .order_by(GeographicCell.last_measurement_at.desc().nulls_last()).limit(limit).offset(offset))).all()
    return Page(items=[CellOut(h3_index=h, center_lat=la, center_lon=lo, last_measurement_at=last)
                       for h, la, lo, last in rows], total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset)


# ----- algorithme de collecte
@router.get("/collection/algorithm", response_model=AlgorithmStateOut)
async def algorithm(admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    state = await db.get(TenantAlgorithmState, admin.tenant_id)
    return AlgorithmStateOut(weights=await collection_service.get_weights(db, admin.tenant_id),
                             base_weights=collection_service.base_weights(),
                             samples_used=state.samples_used if state else 0,
                             updated_at=state.updated_at if state else None)


@router.post("/collection/algorithm/reset", response_model=Msg)
async def reset_algorithm(request: Request, admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await collection_service.reset_weights(db, admin.tenant_id)
    await audit(db, admin, "algorithm.reset", "algorithm", admin.tenant_id, request=request)
    await db.commit()
    return Msg(detail="weights_reset")


# ----- qualité
@router.get("/quality/summary", response_model=QualitySummaryOut)
async def quality_summary(admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    by_status = dict((await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality.status, func.count()).where(
        MeasurementQuality.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id).group_by(MeasurementQuality.status))).all())
    reasons = (await db.execute(text(
        "SELECT r->>'code' AS code, count(*) AS n FROM measurement_quality q, "
        "jsonb_array_elements(q.reasons) r WHERE q.tenant_id = :tid GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 20"),
        {"tid": admin.tenant_id})).all()
    return QualitySummaryOut(by_status={k.value: v for k, v in by_status.items()},
                             top_reason_codes={code: n for code, n in reasons})


# ----- paramètres du tenant
@router.get("/settings", response_model=SettingsOverrides)
async def get_settings(admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, admin.tenant_id)
    return SettingsOverrides(**(tenant.settings or {}))


@router.put("/settings", response_model=SettingsOverrides)
async def put_settings(payload: SettingsOverrides, request: Request, admin: User = Depends(_admin),
                       db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, admin.tenant_id)
    values = payload.model_dump(exclude_none=True)
    from app.services.thresholds import build_thresholds
    th = build_thresholds(values)
    if th.indicative_min_score > th.validated_min_score:
        raise HTTPException(422, "indicative_min_score_must_be_lte_validated_min_score")
    tenant.settings = values
    await audit(db, admin, "settings.update", "tenant", tenant.id, values, request)
    await db.commit()
    return payload


# ----- journal d'audit
@router.get("/audit-logs", response_model=Page[AuditLogOut])
async def audit_logs(limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500), offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
                     admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(AuditLog).where(
        AuditLog.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id))).scalar_one()
    rows = (await db.execute(select(AuditLog).where(AuditLog.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id)
                             .order_by(AuditLog.created_at.desc()).limit(limit).offset(offset))).scalars().all()
    return Page(items=rows, total=total, limit=limit, offset=offset)

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.insights import AIInsight
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter,CellAnalysis, InsightOut
from app.services import ai as ai_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/ai", tags=["ai"])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)


@router.post("/cells/{h3_index}/analysis", response_model=CellAnalysis,
             dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("ai", settings.RATE_LIMIT_AI_PER_MINUTE))])
async def analyze(h3_index: str, tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), user: User = Depends(_staff),
                  db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    result = await ai_service.analyze_cell(db, redis, user.tenant_id, h3_index, tf)
    return result if isinstance(result, dict) else result.content


@router.get("/insights", response_model=list[InsightOut])
async def insights(h3_index: str | None = None, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
                   user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    q = select(AIInsight).where(AIInsight.tenant_id == user.tenant_id)
    if h3_index:
        q = q.where(AIInsight.h3_index == h3_index)
    return (await db.execute(q.order_by(AIInsight.created_at.desc()).limit(limit))).scalars().all()

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request, status
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.account_settings import account_settings as acs
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import Principal, get_current_user, get_db, get_principal, get_public_db
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_ip
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.accounts import (LoginIn, PasswordChangeIn, PasswordConfirmIn, PasswordResetIn, RegisterIn,
                                  RegisterOut)
from app.schemas.common import Msg
from app.schemas.schemas import LogoutRequest, RefreshRequest, TokenPair
from app.services import auth as auth_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["auth"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_ip("auth", settings.RATE_LIMIT_AUTH_PER_MINUTE))])


def _ip(request: Request) -> str | None:
    return request.client.host if request.client else None


@router.post("/register", response_model=RegisterOut, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def register(payload: RegisterIn, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_public_db)):
    user, profile, codes = await auth_service.register(db, payload)
    return RegisterOut(id=user.id, pseudonym=user.pseudonym, display_name=profile.display_name, role=user.role,
                       created_at=user.created_at, recovery_codes=codes)


@router.post("/login", response_model=TokenPair)
async def login(payload: LoginIn, request: Request, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_public_db),
                redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await auth_service.login(db, redis, payload, _ip(request), request.headers.get("user-agent"))


@router.post("/refresh", response_model=TokenPair)
async def refresh(payload: RefreshRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_public_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await auth_service.refresh(db, redis, payload.refresh_token)


@router.post("/logout", response_model=Msg)
async def logout(payload: LogoutRequest, principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal),
                 redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    await auth_service.logout(redis, principal, payload.refresh_token)
    return Msg(detail="logged_out")


@router.post("/logout-all", response_model=Msg)
async def logout_all(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    await auth_service.logout_all(redis, principal)
    return Msg(detail="all_sessions_revoked")


@router.post("/password/change", response_model=Msg)
async def password_change(payload: PasswordChangeIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                          db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    await auth_service.change_password(db, redis, user, payload.current_password, payload.new_password, request)
    return Msg(detail="password_changed")


@router.post("/password/reset", response_model=Msg,
             dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_ip("pwreset", acs.RATE_LIMIT_PWRESET_PER_MINUTE))])
async def password_reset(payload: PasswordResetIn, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_public_db),
                         redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    await auth_service.reset_password(db, redis, payload.email, payload.recovery_code, payload.new_password)
    return Msg(detail="password_reset")


@router.post("/recovery-codes", response_model=list[str])
async def recovery_codes(payload: PasswordConfirmIn, user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                         db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Régénère les codes de récupération (les anciens sont invalidés)."""
    return await auth_service.regenerate_recovery_codes(db, user, payload.password)

# FICHIER: routers/health.py
################################################################################

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, HTTPException, Response
from prometheus_client import CONTENT_TYPE_LATEST, generate_latest
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import text

import hmac

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.db.session import SessionLocal

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/health", include_in_schema=False)
async def health():
    return {"status": "ok"}


@router.get("/ready", include_in_schema=False)
async def ready(redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    try:
        async with SessionLocal() as s:
            await s.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
        await redis.ping()
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(503, "not_ready")
    return {"status": "ready"}


@router.get("/metrics", include_in_schema=False)
async def metrics(authorization: str = Header(default="")):
    expected = "Bearer " + settings.METRICS_TOKEN.get_secret_value()
    if not hmac.compare_digest(authorization, expected):
        raise HTTPException(403, "forbidden")
    return Response(generate_latest(), media_type=CONTENT_TYPE_LATEST)

################################################################################
# FICHIER: routers/map.py
################################################################################

import hashlib
import json
import math

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db
from app.core.field_settings import field_settings as fs
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services import map as map_service
from app.services import noise_field as field_service
from app.services import timelapse as timelapse_service
from app.services.thresholds import build_thresholds
from typing import Literal

router = APIRouter(prefix="/map", tags=["map"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("map", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])


def _parse_bbox(bbox: str | None):
    if not bbox:
        return None
    try:
        a = tuple(float(x) for x in bbox.split(","))
        assert len(a) == 4 and -180 <= a[0] < a[2] <= 180 and -90 <= a[1] < a[3] <= 90
        return a
    except (ValueError, AssertionError):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_bbox (min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat)")


def _snap(bbox, step: float = 0.01):
    if bbox is None:
        return None
    return (math.floor(bbox[0] / step) * step, math.floor(bbox[1] / step) * step,
            math.ceil(bbox[2] / step) * step, math.ceil(bbox[3] / step) * step)


async def public_min_devices(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> int:
    """Même seuil pour tout le monde (tous rôles) : la carte générale est unique et vient du serveur."""
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, user.tenant_id)
    return build_thresholds(tenant.settings).public_min_devices


async def _cached(redis: Redis, raw: list, ttl: int, loader):
    key = "map:" + hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(raw, default=str).encode()).hexdigest()
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)
    data = await loader()
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(data, default=str), ex=ttl)
    return data


@router.get("/noise")
async def noise(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False),
                min_confidence: float = Query(0.0, ge=0, le=1), bbox: str | None = Query(None),
                user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    box = _snap(_parse_bbox(bbox))
    min_dev = await public_min_devices(db, user)
    raw = [str(user.tenant_id), "noise", tf.period.value, str(tf.date_from), str(tf.date_to), tf.day_part,
           include_indicative, min_confidence, box, min_dev]
    return await _cached(redis, raw, fs.FIELD_MAP_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, lambda: map_service.noise_geojson(
        db, user.tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, min_confidence, min_dev, box))


@router.get("/field")
async def field(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False), bbox: str = Query(...),
                user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Champ de bruit continu (nuage) poussé par le vent : calculé une seule fois côté serveur pour tous."""
    return await field_service.build(db, redis, user.tenant_id, tf, include_indicative,
                                     await public_min_devices(db, user), _parse_bbox(bbox))


@router.get("/timelapse")
async def timelapse(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), step: Literal["day", "daypart"] = Query("day"),
                    include_indicative: bool = Query(False), bbox: str | None = Query(None),
                    user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                    redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await timelapse_service.build(db, redis, user.tenant_id, tf, step, include_indicative,
                                         _parse_bbox(bbox), await public_min_devices(db, user))


@router.get("/cells/{h3_index}")
async def cell(h3_index: str, tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False),
               user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    detail = await map_service.cell_detail(db, user.tenant_id, h3_index, tf, include_indicative,
                                           await public_min_devices(db, user))
    if detail is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "insufficient_data")
    return detail

# FICHIER: routers/missions.py

import uuid
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.enums import MissionStatus, Role
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.quota import MissionOut, MissionSyncIn, MissionTarget
from app.services import missions as missions_service
from app.services import tomtom as tt

router = APIRouter(prefix="/missions", tags=["missions"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("missions", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)


@router.get("", response_model=list[MissionOut])
async def list_missions(user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await missions_service.reconcile(db, user.tenant_id, user.id)
    await db.commit()
    return await missions_service.list_for_user(db, user)


@router.post("/sync", response_model=list[MissionOut])
async def sync(payload: MissionSyncIn, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
               redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    await missions_service.reconcile(db, user.tenant_id, user.id)
    await missions_service.sync_for_user(db, redis, user, payload.latitude, payload.longitude)
    await db.commit()
    return await missions_service.list_for_user(db, user)


@router.post("/{mission_id}/accept", response_model=MissionOut)
async def accept(mission_id: uuid.UUID, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await missions_service.respond(db, user, mission_id, True)


@router.post("/{mission_id}/decline", response_model=MissionOut)
async def decline(mission_id: uuid.UUID, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await missions_service.respond(db, user, mission_id, False)


@router.get("/{mission_id}/route",
            dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("tomtom", settings.RATE_LIMIT_TOMTOM_PER_MINUTE))])
async def mission_route(mission_id: uuid.UUID, o_lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90),
                        o_lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                        mode: Literal["car", "pedestrian", "bicycle", "motorcycle", "taxi", "bus"] = "car",
                        user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Itinéraire (TomTom) de la position de l'utilisateur jusqu'au point cible de la mission."""
    m = await db.get(Mission, mission_id)
    if m is None or m.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "mission_not_found")
    if m.status not in (MissionStatus.offered, MissionStatus.accepted):
        raise HTTPException(409, "mission_closed")
    route = await tt.route(o_lat, o_lon, m.center_lat, m.center_lon, mode)
    target = MissionTarget(lat=m.center_lat, lon=m.center_lon, radius_m=m.radius_m, day_part=m.target_day_part,
                           window_start_at=m.window_start_at, window_end_at=m.window_end_at,
                           segment_id=m.road_segment_id, road_name=m.road_name, road_class=m.road_class)
    return {"mission_id": str(m.id), "target": target, "route": route}


@router.get("/ranking/active")
async def ranking(user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Utilisateurs les plus actifs / fiables (pseudonymes uniquement)."""
    return await missions_service.ranking(db, user.tenant_id)


@router.post("/generate")
async def generate(user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Déclenche immédiatement la génération de missions pour le tenant."""
    created = await missions_service.generate_for_tenant(db, redis, user.tenant_id)
    await db.commit()
    return {"created": created}

################################################################################
# FICHIER: routers/quota.py
################################################################################

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.collection import CollectionProposal
from app.models.enums import ProposalStatus, Role
from app.models.quota import UserQuota
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.quota import QuotaCheckIn, QuotaCheckOut, QuotaOut
from app.services import quota as quota_service
from datetime import datetime, timezone

router = APIRouter(prefix="/quota", tags=["quota"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("quota", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)


@router.get("/me", response_model=QuotaOut)
async def me(user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
             redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    out = await quota_service.state(db, redis, user)
    await db.commit()
    return out


@router.post("/check", response_model=QuotaCheckOut)
async def check(payload: QuotaCheckIn, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Pré-contrôle avant de démarrer une mesure (sans débit). Le serveur reste l'arbitre à l'envoi."""
    free = False
    if payload.proposal_id is not None:
        p = await db.get(CollectionProposal, payload.proposal_id)
        free = p is not None and p.user_id == user.id and p.status == ProposalStatus.accepted
    d = await quota_service.evaluate(db, redis, user, payload.latitude, payload.longitude,
                                     datetime.now(timezone.utc), proposal=free, hard=True, consume=False)
    await db.commit()
    return QuotaCheckOut(allowed=d.allowed, reason=d.reason, cost=round(d.cost, 2), balance=round(d.balance, 2),
                         cooldown_minutes=round(d.cooldown_minutes, 1), retry_at=d.retry_at,
                         cell_need=round(d.cell_need, 3), free=d.free)


@router.get("/overview")
async def overview(user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                   redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    ctx = await quota_service.global_context(db, redis, user.tenant_id, datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    n, avg_bal, avg_target = (await db.execute(
        select(func.count(), func.avg(UserQuota.balance), func.avg(UserQuota.daily_target)).where(
            UserQuota.tenant_id == user.tenant_id))).one()
    return {"context": ctx, "users": int(n), "avg_balance": round(float(avg_bal or 0), 2),
            "avg_daily_target": round(float(avg_target or 0), 2)}

# FICHIER: routers/reports.py
################################################################################

import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Request, Response
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.models.enums import ReportStatus, Role
from app.models.insights import Report
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.schemas import ReportCreate, ReportOut
from app.services.audit import audit
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

router = APIRouter(prefix="/reports", tags=["reports"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("reports", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)


@router.post("", response_model=ReportOut, status_code=202)
async def create(payload: ReportCreate, request: Request, user: User = Depends(_staff),
                 db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    params = payload.model_dump(mode="json", exclude={"kind"})
    report = Report(tenant_id=user.tenant_id, requested_by=user.id, kind=payload.kind,
                    status=ReportStatus.pending, params=params)
    db.add(report)
    await audit(db, user, "report.create", "report", report.id, {"kind": payload.kind.value}, request)
    await db.commit()
    celery_app.send_task("reports.generate", args=[str(user.tenant_id), str(report.id)])
    return report


@router.get("", response_model=list[ReportOut])
async def list_reports(limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200), user: User = Depends(_staff),
                       db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return (await db.execute(select(Report).where(Report.tenant_id == user.tenant_id)
                             .order_by(Report.created_at.desc()).limit(limit))).scalars().all()


@router.get("/{report_id}", response_model=ReportOut)
async def get_report(report_id: uuid.UUID, user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    report = await db.get(Report, report_id)
    if report is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "report_not_found")
    return report


@router.get("/{report_id}/download")
async def download(report_id: uuid.UUID, request: Request, user: User = Depends(_staff),
                   db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    report = await db.get(Report, report_id)
    if report is None or report.status != ReportStatus.done or report.content is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "report_not_available")
    await audit(db, user, "report.download", "report", report.id, request=request)
    await db.commit()
    return Response(report.content, media_type=report.content_type,
                    headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="{report.filename}"'})

import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_roles

from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.social import UserProfile
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.accounts import PresenceIn, SuspendIn, VoteIn
from app.schemas.common import Msg
from app.services import reviews as rv
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from fastapi import HTTPException

router = APIRouter(tags=["reviews"], dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("reviews", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
_admin = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin)


@router.get("/analyst/me")

@router.post("/analyst/disable", response_model=Msg)

@router.post("/presence")

                   redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Battement de présence d'un analyseur (position éphémère, 3 min) : renvoie le nombre d'avis en attente."""
    return {"pending": await rv.heartbeat(db, redis, user, payload.latitude, payload.longitude)}


@router.get("/reviews/pending")
async def pending(user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await rv.pending_for(db, user)

                  db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):

    return await rv.analysts_admin(db, admin.tenant_id)


@router.post("/analyst/admin/{user_id}/suspend", response_model=Msg)
async def admin_suspend(user_id: uuid.UUID, payload: SuspendIn, admin: User = Depends(_admin),
                        db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    p = await db.get(UserProfile, user_id)
    if p is None or p.tenant_id != admin.tenant_id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "user_not_found")
    p.analyst_suspended_until = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=payload.days)
    await db.commit()
    return Msg(detail="suspended")


@router.post("/analyst/admin/{user_id}/reinstate", response_model=Msg)
async def admin_reinstate(user_id: uuid.UUID, admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    p = await db.get(UserProfile, user_id)
    if p is None or p.tenant_id != admin.tenant_id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "user_not_found")
    p.analyst_suspended_until = None
    await db.commit()
    return Msg(detail="reinstated")

# FICHIER: routers/roads.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Request, Response
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_roles, verify_platform_key
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings as rs
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services import road_missions
from app.services import roads as roads_service
from app.services.thresholds import build_thresholds
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

router = APIRouter(prefix="/roads", tags=["roads"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("roads", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
platform_router = APIRouter(prefix="/platform/roads", tags=["platform"], dependencies=[Depends(verify_platform_key)])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)


def _parse_bbox(bbox: str) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    try:
        a = tuple(float(x) for x in bbox.split(","))
        assert len(a) == 4 and -180 <= a[0] < a[2] <= 180 and -90 <= a[1] < a[3] <= 90
        return a
    except (ValueError, AssertionError):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_bbox (min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat)")


async def _min_devices(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> int:
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, user.tenant_id)
    return build_thresholds(tenant.settings).public_min_devices


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ carte

@router.get("/network")
async def network(request: Request, bbox: str = Query(..., description="min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat"),
                  zoom: float | None = Query(None, ge=0, le=22), max_rank: int | None = Query(None, ge=1, le=6),
                  user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                  redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Tracé réel et connecté des routes (statique, mis en cache côté serveur et côté client via ETag)."""
    box, rank = _parse_bbox(bbox), roads_service.rank_for(zoom, max_rank)
    data = await roads_service.network_features(db, redis, box, rank)
    headers = {"ETag": data["etag"], "Cache-Control": f"private, max-age={rs.ROAD_NETWORK_CLIENT_MAX_AGE}"}
    if request.headers.get("if-none-match") == data["etag"]:
        return Response(status_code=304, headers=headers)
    return JSONResponse({"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": data["features"], "truncated": data["truncated"]},
                        headers=headers)


@router.get("/state")
async def state(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False),
                bbox: str = Query(...), zoom: float | None = Query(None, ge=0, le=22),
                max_rank: int | None = Query(None, ge=1, le=6), user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """État de bruit des tronçons mesurés (id -> état). Tout tronçon absent est au niveau de base : calme, vert."""
    box, rank = _parse_bbox(bbox), roads_service.rank_for(zoom, max_rank)
    states = await roads_service.cached_states(db, redis, user.tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, box, rank,
                                               await _min_devices(db, user))
    return {"baseline": roads_service.baseline_state(), "segments": states}


@router.get("/map")
async def road_map(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False),
                   bbox: str = Query(...), zoom: float | None = Query(None, ge=0, le=22),
                   max_rank: int | None = Query(None, ge=1, le=6), user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                   db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    """Réseau + état de bruit fusionnés (GeoJSON prêt à afficher)."""
    box, rank = _parse_bbox(bbox), roads_service.rank_for(zoom, max_rank)
    net = await roads_service.network_features(db, redis, box, rank)
    states = await roads_service.cached_states(db, redis, user.tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, box, rank,
                                               await _min_devices(db, user))
    return roads_service.merge(net, states)


@router.get("/segments/{segment_id}")
async def segment(segment_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), include_indicative: bool = Query(False),
                  user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await roads_service.segment_detail(db, user.tenant_id, segment_id, tf, include_indicative,
                                              await _min_devices(db, user))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ staff

@router.get("/stats")
async def stats(user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
                redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return {"network": await roads_service.import_status(db, redis),
            "coverage": await roads_service.stats(db, user.tenant_id)}


@router.get("/needs")
async def needs(lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90), lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                radius_km: float = Query(5, gt=0, le=15), limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
                user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Routes qui manquent le plus de mesures autour d'un point, avec la plage horaire conseillée."""
    return await road_missions.needs_near(db, user.tenant_id, lat, lon, radius_km, limit)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ plateforme (clé X-Platform-Key)

async def _public_db():
    from app.db.session import SessionLocal
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        yield session


@platform_router.get("/import")
async def import_progress(db: AsyncSession = Depends(_public_db), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await roads_service.import_status(db, redis)


@platform_router.post("/import", status_code=202)
async def import_start(force: bool = Query(False)):
    """Lance (ou relance) l'import du réseau routier ; force=true relance aussi les tuiles récentes."""
    celery_app.send_task("roads.ensure_network", args=[force])
    return {"detail": "import_scheduled", "at": datetime.now(timezone.utc)}

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

# FICHIER: routers/tomtom.py
################################################################################

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Response
from pydantic import BaseModel
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user
from app.core.rate_limit import _hit, limit_by_user
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services import tomtom as tt
from app.services.geo import cell_center, is_valid_cell

router = APIRouter(prefix="/tomtom", tags=["tomtom"])
_rl = Depends(limit_by_user("tomtom", settings.RATE_LIMIT_TOMTOM_PER_MINUTE))


class TileSessionOut(BaseModel):
    expires_at: datetime
    layers: dict[str, str]


class TrafficFlowOut(BaseModel):
    current_speed_kmh: float
    free_flow_speed_kmh: float
    current_travel_time_s: int | None = None
    free_flow_travel_time_s: int | None = None
    confidence: float | None = None
    road_closure: bool
    congestion: float
    level: Literal["free", "moderate", "heavy"]


class IncidentOut(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    category: int | None = None
    delay_magnitude: int | None = None
    description: str | None = None
    from_: str | None = None
    to: str | None = None
    delay_s: int | None = None
    length_m: int | None = None
    road_numbers: list[str] = []
    start: str | None = None
    end: str | None = None
    lat: float
    lon: float


class PlaceOut(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    name: str
    address: str | None = None
    municipality: str | None = None
    category: str | None = None
    lat: float
    lon: float
    distance_m: float | None = None


class AddressOut(BaseModel):
    address: str | None = None
    street: str | None = None
    municipality: str | None = None
    country: str | None = None


class RouteOut(BaseModel):
    length_m: int | None = None
    travel_time_s: int | None = None
    traffic_delay_s: int | None = None
    departure: str | None = None
    arrival: str | None = None
    points: list[list[float]]


def _parse_bbox(bbox: str) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    try:
        a = tuple(float(x) for x in bbox.split(","))
        assert len(a) == 4 and -180 <= a[0] < a[2] <= 180 and -90 <= a[1] < a[3] <= 90
        return a
    except (ValueError, AssertionError):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_bbox")


@router.get("/tile-session", response_model=TileSessionOut, dependencies=[_rl])
async def tile_session(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return tt.issue_tile_session(user.id, user.tenant_id)


@router.get("/tiles/{layer}/{z}/{x}/{y}.png", include_in_schema=False)
async def tile(layer: str, z: int, x: int, y: int, t: str = Query(..., min_length=20),
               redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    claims = tt.verify_tile_token(t)
    await _hit(redis, f"rl:tiles:{claims['sub']}", settings.RATE_LIMIT_TILES_PER_MINUTE)
    content = await tt.fetch_tile(layer, z, x, y)
    if content is None:
        return Response(status_code=204)
    return Response(content, media_type="image/png", headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=60"})


@router.get("/traffic/flow", response_model=TrafficFlowOut, dependencies=[_rl])
async def traffic_flow(lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90), lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                       user: User = Depends(get_current_user), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await tt.flow_point(redis, lat, lon)


@router.get("/traffic/incidents", response_model=list[IncidentOut], dependencies=[_rl])
async def traffic_incidents(bbox: str = Query(..., description="min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat"),
                            user: User = Depends(get_current_user), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    items = await tt.incidents(redis, _parse_bbox(bbox))
    return [{**i, "from_": i.get("from")} for i in items]


@router.get("/cells/{h3_index}/traffic", response_model=TrafficFlowOut, dependencies=[_rl])
async def cell_traffic(h3_index: str, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    if not is_valid_cell(h3_index):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_h3_index")
    lat, lon = cell_center(h3_index)
    return await tt.flow_point(redis, lat, lon)


@router.get("/search", response_model=list[PlaceOut], dependencies=[_rl])
async def search(q: str = Query(..., min_length=2, max_length=120), lat: float | None = Query(None, ge=-90, le=90),
                 lon: float | None = Query(None, ge=-180, le=180), limit: int = Query(8, ge=1, le=20),
                 user: User = Depends(get_current_user), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await tt.search(redis, q, lat, lon, limit)


@router.get("/reverse", response_model=AddressOut, dependencies=[_rl])
async def reverse(lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90), lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                  user: User = Depends(get_current_user), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)):
    return await tt.reverse_geocode(redis, lat, lon)


@router.get("/route", response_model=RouteOut, dependencies=[_rl])
async def route(o_lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90), o_lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                d_lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90), d_lon: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
                mode: Literal["car", "pedestrian", "bicycle", "motorcycle", "taxi", "bus"] = "car",
                user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return await tt.route(o_lat, o_lon, d_lat, d_lon, mode)

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User
from app.schemas.common import Msg
from app.schemas.schemas import (AccountDeleteRequest, ConsentOut, ConsentUpdate, ContributionsOut, TimeFilter,
                                 UserMe, UserUpdate)
from app.services import impact as impact_service
from app.services import user as user_service
from app.services.thresholds import build_thresholds

router = APIRouter(prefix="/users", tags=["users"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("users", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])


@router.get("/me", response_model=UserMe)
async def me(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return user


@router.patch("/me", response_model=UserMe)
async def update_me(payload: UserUpdate, user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    for k, v in payload.model_dump(exclude_none=True).items():
        setattr(user, k, v)
    await db.commit()
    return user


@router.get("/me/consents", response_model=list[ConsentOut])
async def consents(user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await user_service.list_consents(db, user)


@router.put("/me/consents", response_model=list[ConsentOut])
async def set_consent(payload: ConsentUpdate, user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                      db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await user_service.set_consent(db, user, payload)


@router.get("/me/contributions", response_model=ContributionsOut)
async def contributions(user: User = Depends(get_current_user), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    return await user_service.contributions(db, user)


@router.get("/me/impact")
async def impact(tf: TimeFilter = Depends(), user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                 db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Mes mesures et leur effet sur la carte générale (qui, elle, reste unique pour tous)."""
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, user.tenant_id)
    return await impact_service.my_impact(db, user, tf, build_thresholds(tenant.settings).public_min_devices)


@router.delete("/me", response_model=Msg)
async def erase(payload: AccountDeleteRequest, user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
                db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    await user_service.erase_account(db, user, payload.password)
    return Msg(detail="account_erased")

import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from app.services import reviews as reviews_service
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.deps import get_db, require_roles
from app.core.rate_limit import limit_by_user
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, QualityStatus, Role
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services.audit import audit
from app.services.geo import to_local
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

router = APIRouter(prefix="/validation", tags=["validation"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(limit_by_user("validation", settings.RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE))])
_staff = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin, Role.analyst)
_admin = require_roles(Role.tenant_admin)


class BulkIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    ids: list[uuid.UUID] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    status: Literal["validated", "indicative", "rejected"]
    reason: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=500)


class ReleaseIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    ids: list[uuid.UUID] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)


def _item(m: Measurement, q: MeasurementQuality | None) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": str(m.id), "measured_at": m.measured_at, "created_at": m.created_at, "h3_index": m.h3_index,
        "day_part": m.day_part.value, "laeq_db": round(m.laeq + m.correction_db_applied, 1),
        "lmax_db": round(m.lmax + m.correction_db_applied, 1), "lmin_db": round(m.lmin + m.correction_db_applied, 1),
        "duration_seconds": m.duration_seconds, "effective_duration_seconds": m.effective_duration_seconds,
        "gps_accuracy_m": m.gps_accuracy, "saturation_ratio": m.saturation_ratio,
        "moved_distance_m": m.moved_distance_m, "sample_rate_hz": m.sample_rate_hz,
        "noise_context": m.noise_context.value if m.noise_context else None,
        "calibration_status": m.calibration_status.value, "quality_status": m.quality_status.value,
        "quality_score": q.score if q else None, "sub_scores": (q.sub_scores if q else {}) or {},
        "reasons": (q.reasons if q else []) or [], "evaluator": q.evaluator if q else None,
        "contributor_token": m.contributor_hash[:12]}


@router.get("/queue")
async def queue(
        status: list[QualityStatus] | None = Query(None), min_score: float | None = Query(None, ge=0, le=1),
        max_score: float | None = Query(None, ge=0, le=1), calibration: CalibrationStatus | None = None,
        evaluator: Literal["auto", "admin"] | None = None, reason: str | None = Query(None, max_length=60),
        h3_index: str | None = Query(None, max_length=16), date_from: date | None = None, date_to: date | None = None,
        sort: Literal["recent", "score_asc", "score_desc", "laeq_desc"] = "recent",
        limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200), offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
        user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    conds = [Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id]
    if status:
        conds.append(Measurement.quality_status.in_(status))
    if min_score is not None:
        conds.append(MeasurementQuality.score >= min_score)
    if max_score is not None:
        conds.append(MeasurementQuality.score <= max_score)
    if calibration:
        conds.append(Measurement.calibration_status == calibration)
    if evaluator:
        conds.append(MeasurementQuality.evaluator == evaluator)
    if reason:
        conds.append(MeasurementQuality.reasons.contains([{"code": reason}]))
    if h3_index:
        conds.append(Measurement.h3_index == h3_index)
    if date_from:
        conds.append(Measurement.local_date >= date_from)
    if date_to:
        conds.append(Measurement.local_date <= date_to)

    join = Measurement.id == MeasurementQuality.measurement_id
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(Measurement)
                              .outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, join).where(*conds))).scalar_one()
    order = {"recent": Measurement.measured_at.desc(), "score_asc": MeasurementQuality.score.asc().nulls_last(),
             "score_desc": MeasurementQuality.score.desc().nulls_last(), "laeq_desc": Measurement.laeq.desc()}[sort]
    rows = (await db.execute(select(Measurement, MeasurementQuality).outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, join)
                             .where(*conds).order_by(order, Measurement.id).limit(limit).offset(offset))).all()
    return {"items": [_item(m, q) for m, q in rows], "total": total, "limit": limit, "offset": offset}


@router.post("/bulk")
async def bulk(payload: BulkIn, request: Request, admin: User = Depends(_admin), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    status = QualityStatus(payload.status)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ms = (await db.execute(select(Measurement).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id, Measurement.id.in_(payload.ids)).with_for_update())).scalars().all()
    qs = {q.measurement_id: q for q in (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(
        MeasurementQuality.measurement_id.in_([m.id for m in ms])))).scalars()} if ms else {}
    for m in ms:
        q = qs.get(m.id)
        if q is None:
            q = MeasurementQuality(tenant_id=m.tenant_id, measurement_id=m.id, status=status, score=0.0,
                                   sub_scores={}, reasons=[], evaluator="admin", evaluated_at=now)
            db.add(q)
        q.status, q.evaluator, q.override_by, q.evaluated_at = status, "admin", admin.id, now
        q.reasons = [*(q.reasons or []), {"code": "admin_override", "severity": "info", "reason": payload.reason}]
        m.quality_status, m.aggregated_at = status, None            # force le recalcul des agrégats
    await reviews_service.cancel_rounds(db, [m.id for m in ms])
    await audit(db, admin, "validation.bulk", "measurement", None,
                {"count": len(ms), "status": payload.status, "reason": payload.reason}, request)
    await db.commit()
    return {"updated": len(ms), "skipped": len(payload.ids) - len(ms)}


@router.post("/release")
async def release(payload: ReleaseIn, request: Request, admin: User = Depends(_admin),
                  db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Annule la décision admin : la mesure repasse en évaluation automatique."""
    ms = (await db.execute(select(Measurement).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == admin.tenant_id, Measurement.id.in_(payload.ids)).with_for_update())).scalars().all()
    qs = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(
        MeasurementQuality.measurement_id.in_([m.id for m in ms])))).scalars().all() if ms else []
    for q in qs:
        q.evaluator, q.override_by = "auto", None
    for m in ms:
        m.quality_status, m.aggregated_at = QualityStatus.pending, None
    await audit(db, admin, "validation.release", "measurement", None, {"count": len(ms)}, request)
    await db.commit()
    for m in ms:
        celery_app.send_task("measurements.evaluate", args=[str(admin.tenant_id), str(m.id)])
    return {"released": len(ms)}


@router.get("/stats")
async def stats(days: int = Query(14, ge=1, le=90), user: User = Depends(_staff), db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    since = to_local(now).date() - timedelta(days=days - 1)
    per_day: dict = {}
    totals = {s.value: 0 for s in QualityStatus}
    for d, st, n in (await db.execute(
            select(Measurement.local_date, Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(
                Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.local_date >= since)
            .group_by(Measurement.local_date, Measurement.quality_status))).all():
        per_day.setdefault(d.isoformat(), {s.value: 0 for s in QualityStatus})[st.value] = n
        totals[st.value] += n
    stuck = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(Measurement).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.quality_status == QualityStatus.pending,
        Measurement.created_at < now - timedelta(minutes=5)))).scalar_one()
    overrides = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(MeasurementQuality).where(
        MeasurementQuality.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, MeasurementQuality.evaluator == "admin",
        MeasurementQuality.evaluated_at >= now - timedelta(days=days)))).scalar_one()
    reasons = (await db.execute(text(
        "SELECT r->>'code' AS code, count(*) AS n FROM measurement_quality q, jsonb_array_elements(q.reasons) r "
        "WHERE q.tenant_id = :tid AND q.evaluated_at >= :since GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 15"),
        {"tid": user.tenant_id, "since": now - timedelta(days=days)})).all()
    return {"days": [{"date": d, **v} for d, v in sorted(per_day.items())], "totals": totals,
            "pending_stuck": stuck, "admin_overrides": overrides,
            "top_reasons": [{"code": c, "n": int(n)} for c, n in reasons]}

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, EmailStr, Field, field_validator

from app.models.enums import Role


class RegisterIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    email: EmailStr
    password: str = Field(min_length=10, max_length=128)
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=24, pattern=r"^[\w .'-]+$")
    first_name: str | None = Field(None, max_length=60)
    last_name: str | None = Field(None, max_length=60)
    phone: str | None = Field(None, pattern=r"^\+?[0-9 ()-]{6,20}$")
    city: str | None = Field(None, max_length=80)
    district: str | None = Field(None, max_length=80)
    birth_year: int | None = None
    invite_code: str | None = Field(None, max_length=24)
    accept_terms: bool

    @field_validator("birth_year")
    @classmethod
    def _year(cls, v):
        if v is not None and not (1900 <= v <= datetime.now(timezone.utc).year - 13):
            raise ValueError("année de naissance invalide (13 ans minimum)")
        return v

import uuid

    daily_target: float
    rate_per_hour: float
    cap: float
    reliability: float
    utilization: float
    mission_rate: float
    retention_per_day: float
    need_now: float
    projected_24h: float
    today_credited: float
    today_used: float
    today_decayed: float


class QuotaCheckIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    latitude: float = Field(ge=-90, le=90)
    longitude: float = Field(ge=-180, le=180)
    proposal_id: uuid.UUID | None = None


class QuotaCheckOut(BaseModel):
    allowed: bool
    reason: str
    cost: float
    balance: float
    cooldown_minutes: float
    retry_at: datetime | None
    cell_need: float
    free: bool


class MissionTarget(BaseModel):
    """Point précis à rejoindre (trajet calculé côté mobile). Une mesure à moins de radius_m est valide."""
    lat: float
    lon: float
    radius_m: float
    day_part: DayPart | None = None
    window_start_at: datetime | None = None
    window_end_at: datetime | None = None
    segment_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
    road_name: str | None = None
    road_class: str | None = None


class MissionOut(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)
    id: uuid.UUID
    kind: str
    status: MissionStatus
    zone_h3: str
    center_lat: float
    center_lon: float
    radius_m: float
    distance_km: float
    required_count: int
    done_count: int
    confirmed_count: int
    reward_credits: float
    priority: float
    road_segment_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
    road_name: str | None = None
    road_class: str | None = None
    target_day_part: DayPart | None = None
    window_start_at: datetime | None = None
    window_end_at: datetime | None = None
    created_at: datetime
    offer_expires_at: datetime
    accepted_at: datetime | None
    deadline_at: datetime | None
    finished_at: datetime | None

    @computed_field
    @property
    def target(self) -> MissionTarget | None:
        if self.kind != "road":
            return None
        return MissionTarget(lat=self.center_lat, lon=self.center_lon, radius_m=self.radius_m,
                             day_part=self.target_day_part, window_start_at=self.window_start_at,
                             window_end_at=self.window_end_at, segment_id=self.road_segment_id,
                             road_name=self.road_name, road_class=self.road_class)


class MissionSyncIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    latitude: float = Field(ge=-90, le=90)
    longitude: float = Field(ge=-180, le=180)

class ScheduleIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    days_of_week: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=7)
    window_start: time
    window_end: time

    @field_validator("days_of_week")
    @classmethod
    def _days(cls, v: list[int]) -> list[int]:
        if any(d < 0 or d > 6 for d in v) or len(set(v)) != len(v):
            raise ValueError("days_of_week : entiers 0 (lundi) à 6 (dimanche), sans doublon")
        return sorted(v)

    @field_validator("window_start", "window_end", "preferred_times", mode="after")
    @classmethod
    def _naive(cls, v):
        values = v if isinstance(v, list) else [v]
        if any(t.tzinfo is not None for t in values):
            raise ValueError("Les heures doivent être locales, sans fuseau")
        return v

# FICHIER: services/accounts.py
