# FICHIER: services/report.py
################################################################################

import csv
import io
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import ReportKind, ReportStatus
from app.models.insights import Report
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services import ai as ai_service
from app.services.map import cell_rows

COLUMNS = ["h3_index", "laeq_db", "lmax_db", "measurement_count", "device_count", "confidence",
           "calibrated_share", "last_measurement_at"]


def build_csv(rows: list[dict]) -> bytes:
    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf)
    w.writerow(COLUMNS)
    for r in rows:
        w.writerow([r["h3_index"], r["laeq_db"], r["lmax_db"], r["measurement_count"], r["device_count"],
                    r["confidence"], r["calibrated_share"], r["last_measurement_at"].isoformat()])
    return buf.getvalue().encode("utf-8-sig")


def build_pdf(rows: list[dict], params: dict, narrative: dict | None) -> bytes:
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
    from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet
    from reportlab.platypus import Paragraph, SimpleDocTemplate, Spacer, Table, TableStyle

    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(buf, pagesize=A4, title="Rapport SunuSound")
    story = [Paragraph("Rapport SunuSound — cartographie sonore indicative", styles["Title"]),
             Paragraph(f"Période : {params['period']} — plage : {params['day_part']} — "
                       f"indicatives incluses : {'oui' if params['include_indicative'] else 'non'}", styles["Normal"]),
             Paragraph("Mesures participatives : indicatives, non certifiées réglementairement.", styles["Italic"]),
             Spacer(1, 12)]
    if narrative:
        story.append(Paragraph("Synthèse (générée par IA)", styles["Heading2"]))
        story.append(Paragraph(narrative["executive_summary"], styles["Normal"]))
        for title, key in (("Constats", "key_findings"), ("Recommandations", "recommendations"),
                           ("Limites", "limitations")):
            if narrative.get(key):
                story.append(Paragraph(title, styles["Heading3"]))
                for item in narrative[key]:
                    story.append(Paragraph(f"• {item}", styles["Normal"]))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 12))
    data = [["Cellule H3", "LAeq (dB)", "Lmax (dB)", "Mesures", "Appareils", "Confiance"]]
    for r in rows[:2000]:
        data.append([r["h3_index"], r["laeq_db"], r["lmax_db"], r["measurement_count"], r["device_count"], r["confidence"]])
    table = Table(data, repeatRows=1)
    table.setStyle(TableStyle([("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.lightgrey), ("FONTSIZE", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
                               ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.25, colors.grey)]))
    story.append(table)
    doc.build(story)
    return buf.getvalue()


async def generate(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, report_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    report = await db.get(Report, report_id, with_for_update=True)
    if report is None or report.status not in (ReportStatus.pending, ReportStatus.failed):
        return
    report.status = ReportStatus.running
    await db.commit()
    try:
        p = report.params
        tf = TimeFilter(period=p["period"], date_from=_d(p.get("date_from")), date_to=_d(p.get("date_to")),
                        day_part=p["day_part"])
        rows = await cell_rows(db, tenant_id, tf, p["include_indicative"], min_devices=1)
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["laeq_db"], reverse=True)
        if report.kind == ReportKind.csv:
            report.content, report.content_type = build_csv(rows), "text/csv"
            report.filename = f"sunusound-{report.id}.csv"
        else:
            narrative = None
            if p.get("include_ai_summary") and rows:
                narrative = await ai_service.summarize_report(redis, tenant_id, {
                    "period": {"period": p["period"], "day_part": p["day_part"]},
                    "cells_count": len(rows),
                    "top_cells": [{k: r[k] for k in ("laeq_db", "lmax_db", "measurement_count", "device_count", "confidence")}
                                  for r in rows[:20]],
                    "overall_median_laeq_db": sorted(r["laeq_db"] for r in rows)[len(rows) // 2]})
            report.content, report.content_type = build_pdf(rows, p, narrative), "application/pdf"
            report.filename = f"sunusound-{report.id}.pdf"
        report.status = ReportStatus.done
    except Exception as exc:                       # message court, sans donnée sensible
        report.status, report.error = ReportStatus.failed, f"{type(exc).__name__}"
    report.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await db.commit()


def _d(value):
    from datetime import date
    return date.fromisoformat(value) if value else None


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/reviews.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import case, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.account_settings import account_settings as acs
from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import MissionStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.models.social import MeasurementReview, ReviewVote, UserProfile
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services.geo import to_local

_P = "t:{}:presence"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ éligibilité

def _crit(key, label, value, required, reverse=False):
    ok = value <= required if reverse else value >= required
    return {"key": key, "label": label, "value": value, "required": required, "ok": bool(ok), "reverse": reverse}


async def eligibility_bulk(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_ids: list[uuid.UUID], now: datetime) -> dict:
    out = {u: {"v": 0, "n": 0, "reward": 0.0, "last": None, "days": 0, "missions": 0, "created": None} for u in user_ids}
    if not user_ids:
        return out
    since = now - timedelta(days=90)
    reward = {QualityStatus.validated: 1.0, QualityStatus.indicative: 0.5, QualityStatus.rejected: 0.0}
    for uid, st, n in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids), Measurement.measured_at >= since,
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending).group_by(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status))).all():
        out[uid]["n"] += n
        out[uid]["reward"] += reward[st] * n
        out[uid]["v"] += n if st == QualityStatus.validated else 0
    for uid, last in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, func.max(Measurement.measured_at)).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= since).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["last"] = last
    for uid, d in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.local_date))).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=60),
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["days"] = d
    for uid, n in (await db.execute(select(Mission.user_id, func.count()).where(
            Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Mission.status == MissionStatus.completed).group_by(Mission.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["missions"] = n
    for uid, c in (await db.execute(select(User.id, User.created_at).where(User.id.in_(user_ids)))).all():
        out[uid]["created"] = c
    res = {}
    for uid, d in out.items():
        rel = (d["reward"] + 3.0) / (d["n"] + 5.0)
        since_last = (now - d["last"]).days if d["last"] else 999
        crit = [_crit("validated", "Mesures validées (90 j)", d["v"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_VALIDATED),
                _crit("missions", "Missions réussies", d["missions"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_MISSIONS),
                _crit("reliability", "Fiabilité (%)", round(rel * 100), round(acs.ANALYST_MIN_RELIABILITY * 100)),
                _crit("account_days", "Ancienneté du compte (jours)",
                      (now - d["created"]).days if d["created"] else 0, acs.ANALYST_MIN_ACCOUNT_DAYS),
                _crit("active_days", "Jours d'activité (60 j)", d["days"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_ACTIVE_DAYS),
                _crit("recent", "Jours depuis la dernière mesure (maximum)", since_last,
                      acs.ANALYST_MAX_INACTIVE_DAYS, reverse=True)]
        res[uid] = {"eligible": all(c["ok"] for c in crit), "criteria": crit, "reliability": rel}
    return res


async def agreement_bulk(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_ids: list[uuid.UUID], now: datetime) -> dict:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.sum(case((ReviewVote.matched.is_(True), 1), else_=0)),
                                    func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.tenant_id == tenant_id, ReviewVote.reviewer_id.in_(user_ids), ReviewVote.matched.is_not(None),
        ReviewVote.voted_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id))).all()
    return {u: (int(m or 0), int(n)) for u, m, n in rows}


async def analyst_state(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> dict:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    el = (await eligibility_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], now))[user.id]
    m, n = (await agreement_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], now)).get(user.id, (0, 0))
    return {"enabled": p.analyst_enabled, "suspended_until": p.analyst_suspended_until,
            "eligibility": {"eligible": el["eligible"], "criteria": el["criteria"]},
            "stats": {"votes": n, "matched": m, "agreement": round((m + 3) / (n + 5), 3)}}


async def set_analyst(db: AsyncSession, user: User, enable: bool) -> None:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    from app.services.user import latest_consents
    from app.models.enums import ConsentType
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    if enable:
        c = await latest_consents(db, user.id)
        if ConsentType.geolocation not in c or not c[ConsentType.geolocation].granted:
            raise HTTPException(403, "consent_required")
        el = (await eligibility_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], datetime.now(timezone.utc)))[user.id]
        if not el["eligible"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "analyst_not_eligible")
        if not p.analyst_enabled:
            p.analyst_since = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    p.analyst_enabled = enable
    await db.commit()


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ présence (éphémère, Redis)

async def heartbeat(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User, lat: float, lon: float) -> int:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if not p.analyst_enabled or (p.analyst_suspended_until and p.analyst_suspended_until > now):
        raise HTTPException(403, "not_analyst")
    key = _P.format(user.tenant_id)
    pipe = redis.pipeline()
    pipe.geoadd(key, (round(lon, 4), round(lat, 4), str(user.id)))
    pipe.zadd(key + ":ts", {str(user.id): now.timestamp()})
    pipe.expire(key, 3600)
    pipe.expire(key + ":ts", 3600)
    await pipe.execute()
    return (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now))).scalar_one()


async def purge_presence(redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    key = _P.format(tenant_id)
    stale = await redis.zrangebyscore(key + ":ts", 0, datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - acs.PRESENCE_TTL_SECONDS)
    if stale:
        await redis.zrem(key, *stale)
        await redis.zrem(key + ":ts", *stale)


async def _nearby(redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float) -> list[tuple[uuid.UUID, float]]:
    key = _P.format(tenant_id)
    res = await redis.geosearch(key, longitude=lon, latitude=lat, radius=acs.REVIEW_RADIUS_M, unit="m",
                                sort="ASC", count=60, withdist=True)
    if not res:
        return []
    ids = [r[0] for r in res]
    ts = await redis.zmscore(key + ":ts", ids)
    limit = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - acs.PRESENCE_TTL_SECONDS
    return [(uuid.UUID(i), float(d)) for (i, d), t in zip(res, ts) if t is not None and t >= limit]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ envoi des demandes d'avis

async def dispatch(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, measurement_id: uuid.UUID) -> str:
    m = await db.get(Measurement, measurement_id)
    if m is None or m.user_id is None:
        return "gone"
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if now - m.measured_at > timedelta(minutes=acs.REVIEW_MAX_AGE_MINUTES):
        return "stale"                       # on ne juge que le volume du moment
    if m.quality_status not in (QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative):
        return "not_eligible"
    q = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == m.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if q is not None and q.evaluator in ("admin", "peer"):
        return "admin_priority"              # la décision d'un admin prime : aucun avis demandé
    if (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(MeasurementReview.measurement_id == m.id))).first():
        return "exists"
    near = [(u, d) for u, d in await _nearby(redis, tenant_id, m.latitude, m.longitude) if u != m.user_id]
    if len(near) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        return "no_reviewers"
    ids = [u for u, _ in near]
    profiles = {p.user_id: p for p in (await db.execute(select(UserProfile).where(UserProfile.user_id.in_(ids)))).scalars()}
    pend = dict((await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id.in_(ids), ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now)
        .group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id))).all())
    cand = [(u, d) for u, d in near if (p := profiles.get(u)) and p.analyst_enabled
            and not (p.analyst_suspended_until and p.analyst_suspended_until > now)
            and pend.get(u, 0) < acs.REVIEW_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER]
    el = await eligibility_bulk(db, tenant_id, [u for u, _ in cand], now)
    agr = await agreement_bulk(db, tenant_id, [u for u, _ in cand], now)
    scored = []
    for u, d in cand:
        if not el[u]["eligible"]:
            continue
        mt, n = agr.get(u, (0, 0))
        w = (0.5 + el[u]["reliability"]) * (1.0 - 0.4 * d / acs.REVIEW_RADIUS_M) * (0.7 + 0.6 * (mt + 3) / (n + 5))
        scored.append((w, u, d))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)
    chosen = scored[:acs.REVIEW_MAX_REVIEWERS]
    if len(chosen) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        return "no_reviewers"
    exp = now + timedelta(minutes=acs.REVIEW_TTL_MINUTES)
    level = round(m.laeq + m.correction_db_applied, 1)
    review = MeasurementReview(tenant_id=tenant_id, measurement_id=m.id, status="open", requested=len(chosen),
                               expires_at=exp)
    db.add(review)
    await db.flush()
    for w, u, d in chosen:
        db.add(ReviewVote(tenant_id=tenant_id, review_id=review.id, reviewer_id=u, status="pending", level_db=level,
                          measured_at=m.measured_at, distance_m=d, weight=w, expires_at=exp))
    return "dispatched"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ vote et décision

def decide(votes: list[tuple[str, bool, float]]) -> dict:
    tw = sum(w for _, _, w in votes) or 1.0
    a = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "agree") / tw
    d = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "down") / tw
    u = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "up") / tw
    base = {"agree": a, "down": d, "up": u, "adjust": 0.0}
    if a >= acs.REVIEW_CONFIRM_RATIO:
        return {**base, "outcome": "confirmed"}
    direction, ratio = ("down", d) if d >= u else ("up", u)
    if ratio >= acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_RATIO:
        dir_w = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == direction)
        strong_w = sum(w for v, s, w in votes if v == direction and s)
        if dir_w and strong_w / dir_w >= acs.REVIEW_STRONG_REJECT_SHARE:
            return {**base, "outcome": "rejected"}          # écart important : trop grand pour un simple ajustement
        mag = min(acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_MAX_DB, acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_STEP_DB * (1 + 4 * (ratio - 0.5)))
        mag = round(mag * 2) / 2
        return {**base, "outcome": f"adjusted_{direction}", "adjust": -mag if direction == "down" else mag}
    return {**base, "outcome": "rejected"}                  # réponses contradictoires


async def finalize(db: AsyncSession, review_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime) -> str | None:
    r = await db.get(MeasurementReview, review_id, with_for_update=True)
    if r is None or r.status != "open":
        return None
    votes = list((await db.execute(select(ReviewVote).where(ReviewVote.review_id == r.id))).scalars())
    for v in votes:
        if v.status == "pending":
            v.status = "expired"
    voted = [v for v in votes if v.status == "voted"]
    m = await db.get(Measurement, r.measurement_id, with_for_update=True)
    q = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == r.measurement_id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    r.status, r.closed_at, r.votes = "closed", now, len(voted)
    if m is None or q is None or q.evaluator == "admin":
        r.outcome = "admin_skip"
        return r.outcome
    if len(voted) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        r.outcome = "no_quorum"
        return r.outcome
    res = decide([(v.vote, v.strong, v.weight) for v in voted])
    r.outcome, r.adjust_db = res["outcome"], res["adjust"]
    r.agree_ratio, r.down_ratio, r.up_ratio = round(res["agree"], 3), round(res["down"], 3), round(res["up"], 3)
    reason = {"code": f"peer_{res['outcome']}" if res["outcome"] != "confirmed" else "peer_confirmed",
              "severity": "info", "votes": len(voted), "agree": r.agree_ratio, "adjust_db": res["adjust"]}
    if res["outcome"] == "rejected":
        reason["code"], reason["severity"] = "peer_rejected", "reject"
        m.quality_status, q.status, q.score = QualityStatus.rejected, QualityStatus.rejected, 0.0
    else:
        m.quality_status, q.status = QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.validated
        q.score = max(q.score, settings.VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE)
        if res["adjust"]:
            m.correction_db_applied = float(m.correction_db_applied or 0) + res["adjust"]
    q.evaluator, q.override_by, q.evaluated_at = "peer", None, now
    q.reasons = [*(q.reasons or []), reason]
    m.aggregated_at = None                                   # recalcul des agrégats concernés
    for v in voted:
        v.matched = (v.vote == "agree") if res["outcome"] in ("confirmed", "rejected") and res["outcome"] == "confirmed" \
            else (v.vote != "agree") if res["outcome"] == "rejected" else (v.vote == res["outcome"].split("_")[1])
    return r.outcome


async def cast_vote(db: AsyncSession, user: User, vote_id: uuid.UUID, vote: str, strong: bool) -> dict:
    v = await db.get(ReviewVote, vote_id, with_for_update=True)
    if v is None or v.reviewer_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "review_not_found")
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if v.status != "pending" or v.expires_at <= now:
        raise HTTPException(409, "review_expired")
    v.vote, v.strong, v.status, v.voted_at = vote, strong and vote != "agree", "voted", now
    await db.flush()
    left = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.review_id == v.review_id, ReviewVote.status == "pending"))).scalar_one()
    outcome = await finalize(db, v.review_id, now) if left == 0 else None
    await db.commit()
    return {"ok": True, "closed": outcome is not None}


async def close_expired(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ids = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(
        MeasurementReview.tenant_id == tenant_id, MeasurementReview.status == "open",
        MeasurementReview.expires_at <= now).limit(200))).scalars().all()
    for rid in ids:
        await finalize(db, rid, now)
    await purge_presence(redis, tenant_id)
    return len(ids)


async def cancel_rounds(db: AsyncSession, measurement_ids: list[uuid.UUID]) -> None:
    """Une décision admin saute le processus d'avis : manches ouvertes closes, demandes annulées."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rids = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(
        MeasurementReview.measurement_id.in_(measurement_ids), MeasurementReview.status == "open"))).scalars().all()
    if rids:
        await db.execute(update(ReviewVote).where(ReviewVote.review_id.in_(rids), ReviewVote.status == "pending")
                         .values(status="cancelled"))
        await db.execute(update(MeasurementReview).where(MeasurementReview.id.in_(rids))
                         .values(status="closed", outcome="admin_skip", closed_at=now))


async def audit_analysts(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.sum(case((ReviewVote.matched.is_(True), 1), else_=0)),
                                    func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.tenant_id == tenant_id, ReviewVote.matched.is_not(None),
        ReviewVote.voted_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id)
        .having(func.count() >= acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_MIN_VOTES))).all()
    bad = [u for u, m, n in rows if (m or 0) / n < acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_AGREEMENT]
    if not bad:
        return 0
    res = await db.execute(update(UserProfile).where(
        UserProfile.user_id.in_(bad), UserProfile.analyst_enabled.is_(True),
        (UserProfile.analyst_suspended_until.is_(None)) | (UserProfile.analyst_suspended_until < now))
        .values(analyst_suspended_until=now + timedelta(days=acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_DAYS)))
    return res.rowcount or 0


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ lecture

async def pending_for(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> list[dict]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now)
        .order_by(ReviewVote.expires_at))).scalars().all()
    return [{"id": str(v.id), "level_db": v.level_db, "distance_m": round(v.distance_m, -1),
             "measured_at": v.measured_at, "expires_at": v.expires_at} for v in rows]


async def history_for(db: AsyncSession, user: User, limit: int) -> list[dict]:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote, MeasurementReview.outcome)
                             .join(MeasurementReview, MeasurementReview.id == ReviewVote.review_id)
                             .where(ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status.in_(["voted", "expired"]))
                             .order_by(ReviewVote.created_at.desc()).limit(limit))).all()
    return [{"id": str(v.id), "vote": v.vote, "strong": v.strong, "level_db": v.level_db, "outcome": o,
             "matched": v.matched, "voted_at": v.voted_at or v.expires_at, "status": v.status} for v, o in rows]


async def analysts_admin(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[dict]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(UserProfile, User.pseudonym).join(User, User.id == UserProfile.user_id).where(
        UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id, UserProfile.analyst_enabled.is_(True)))).all()
    agr = await agreement_bulk(db, tenant_id, [p.user_id for p, _ in rows], now) if rows else {}
    return [{"user_id": str(p.user_id), "pseudonym": ps, "display_name": p.display_name, "since": p.analyst_since,
             "suspended_until": p.analyst_suspended_until, "votes": agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[1],
             "agreement": round((agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[0] + 3) / (agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[1] + 5), 3)}
            for p, ps in rows]

# FICHIER: services/road_missions.py
################################################################################

import math
import uuid
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta, timezone

import h3
from sqlalchemy import or_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings as rs
from app.models.enums import DayPart, MissionStatus
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.geo import TZ, to_local
from app.services.quota import haversine_m

_ACTIVE = (MissionStatus.offered, MissionStatus.accepted)

_CAND = text("""
SELECT s.id, s.highway, s.name, s.ref, s.road_rank, s.class_weight, s.length_m, s.mid_lat, s.mid_lon
FROM road_segments s
WHERE s.active AND s.road_rank <= :max_rank
  AND ST_DWithin(s.geom, ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(:lon, :lat), 4326), :deg)
ORDER BY s.class_weight DESC, s.length_m DESC
LIMIT :lim""")

_COUNTS = text("""
SELECT m.road_segment_id, m.day_part, count(*) AS n
FROM measurements m JOIN road_segments s ON s.id = m.road_segment_id
WHERE m.tenant_id = :tid AND m.measured_at >= :since AND m.quality_status IN ('validated', 'indicative')
  AND s.active AND s.road_rank <= :max_rank
  AND ST_DWithin(s.geom, ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(:lon, :lat), 4326), :deg)
GROUP BY m.road_segment_id, m.day_part""")


@dataclass
class Candidate:
    id: uuid.UUID
    highway: str
    name: str | None
    ref: str | None
    rank: int
    weight: float
    length_m: float
    lat: float
    lon: float
    counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ créneaux horaires

def _bounds() -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
    return {"morning": (settings.DAYPART_MORNING_START, settings.DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START),
            "afternoon": (settings.DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START, settings.DAYPART_EVENING_START),
            "evening": (settings.DAYPART_EVENING_START, settings.DAYPART_NIGHT_START),
            "night": (settings.DAYPART_NIGHT_START, settings.DAYPART_MORNING_START)}


def next_window(now: datetime, part: str) -> tuple[datetime, datetime]:
    """Créneau en cours ou prochain créneau de la plage horaire (UTC)."""
    local = to_local(now)
    s_h, e_h = _bounds()[part]
    for off in (-1, 0, 1):
        d0 = local.date() + timedelta(days=off)
        start = datetime.combine(d0, time(s_h), TZ)
        end = datetime.combine(d0 + timedelta(days=1 if e_h <= s_h else 0), time(e_h), TZ)
        if end > local:
            return max(start, local).astimezone(timezone.utc), end.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    raise RuntimeError("window_not_found")


def eligible_windows(now: datetime) -> dict[str, tuple[datetime, datetime]]:
    out = {}
    for part in _bounds():
        start, end = next_window(now, part)
        if start - now > timedelta(hours=rs.ROAD_LOOKAHEAD_HOURS):
            continue
        if end - max(start, now) < timedelta(minutes=rs.ROAD_MIN_WINDOW_MINUTES):
            continue
        out[part] = (start, end)
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ besoins

def slots(c: Candidate, wins: dict) -> list[tuple[float, str]]:
    """Besoin de mesure (0-1) du tronçon pour chaque plage encore atteignable, du plus fort au plus faible."""
    pw = rs.part_weights
    out = []
    for part in wins:
        gap = clamp01(1.0 - c.counts.get(part, 0) / max(1, rs.ROAD_TARGET_PER_PART))
        need = c.weight * pw.get(part, 1.0) * gap
        if need >= rs.ROAD_MIN_NEED:
            out.append((need, part))
    out.sort(reverse=True)
    return out


def _deg(radius_m: float) -> float:
    return radius_m / 111320.0 * 1.6


async def load_candidates(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float, radius_km: float,
                          now: datetime) -> list[Candidate]:
    p = {"max_rank": rs.ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RANK, "lat": lat, "lon": lon, "deg": _deg(radius_km * 1000)}
    rows = (await db.execute(_CAND, {**p, "lim": rs.ROAD_CANDIDATE_LIMIT})).all()
    cands = {r[0]: Candidate(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5], r[6], r[7], r[8]) for r in rows}
    if not cands:
        return []
    since = now - timedelta(days=rs.ROAD_COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS)
    for sid, part, n in (await db.execute(_COUNTS, {**p, "tid": tenant_id, "since": since})).all():
        c = cands.get(sid)
        if c is not None:
            c.counts[str(part)] = int(n)
    return list(cands.values())


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ affectation

def _make(tenant_id, p, c: Candidate, part: str, win, d_km: float, need: float, fit: float) -> Mission:
    start, end = win
    reward = round(rs.ROAD_MISSION_COUNT * rs.ROAD_REWARD_PER_MEASUREMENT * (0.75 + 0.5 * need)
                   * (0.8 + 0.4 * c.weight), 1)
    return Mission(
        tenant_id=tenant_id, user_id=p.user_id, kind="road", status=MissionStatus.offered,
        zone_h3=h3.latlng_to_cell(c.lat, c.lon, settings.MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES),
        center_lat=c.lat, center_lon=c.lon, radius_m=rs.ROAD_TARGET_RADIUS_M, distance_km=round(d_km, 1),
        required_count=rs.ROAD_MISSION_COUNT, done_count=0, confirmed_count=0, reward_credits=reward,
        priority=round(100 * clamp01(0.65 * need + 0.35 * fit), 1),
        features={"segment_need": round(need, 3), "fit": round(fit, 3), "score": round(p.score, 3),
                  "length_m": c.length_m},
        road_segment_id=c.id, road_name=c.name or c.ref, road_class=c.highway, target_day_part=DayPart(part),
        window_start_at=start, window_end_at=end, offer_expires_at=end)


async def assign(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, profs: dict, now: datetime, max_new: int) -> list[Mission]:
    """Propose à chaque utilisateur (position connue) des points précis sur des routes qui manquent de mesures
    pour une plage horaire atteignable. Les profils sont typés comme missions.Profile (user_id, anchor, score, load)."""
    elig = [p for p in profs.values() if p.anchor is not None and p.score >= settings.MISSION_MIN_SCORE]
    wins = eligible_windows(now)
    if not elig or not wins:
        return []
    rows = (await db.execute(select(Mission.user_id, Mission.road_segment_id, Mission.target_day_part,
                                    Mission.status).where(
        Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.kind == "road",
        or_(Mission.status.in_(_ACTIVE),
            Mission.created_at >= now - timedelta(hours=rs.ROAD_REPEAT_COOLDOWN_HOURS))))).all()
    committed: set = set()
    pairs: set = set()
    road_load: dict = defaultdict(int)
    for uid, sid, part, st in rows:
        pairs.add((uid, sid))
        if st in _ACTIVE:
            committed.add((sid, part.value if part else None))
            road_load[uid] += 1

    radius_km = rs.ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM
    edge_km = h3.average_hexagon_edge_length(rs.ROAD_GROUP_H3_RES, unit="km")
    cache: dict = {}
    created: list[Mission] = []
    for p in sorted(elig, key=lambda x: x.score, reverse=True)[:rs.ROAD_MAX_USERS_PER_RUN]:
        uid = p.user_id
        cap = min(max_new, rs.ROAD_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER - road_load[uid], settings.MISSION_MAX_PENDING - p.load)
        if cap <= 0:
            continue
        gk = h3.latlng_to_cell(p.anchor[0], p.anchor[1], rs.ROAD_GROUP_H3_RES)
        if gk not in cache:                                    # une requête par groupe de proximité
            clat, clon = h3.cell_to_latlng(gk)
            cands = await load_candidates(db, tenant_id, clat, clon, radius_km + edge_km + 0.5, now)
            cache[gk] = [(c, s) for c in cands if (s := slots(c, wins))]
        ranked = []
        for c, sl in cache[gk]:
            if (uid, c.id) in pairs:
                continue
            pick = next(((n, part) for n, part in sl if (c.id, part) not in committed), None)
            if pick is None:
                continue
            d_km = haversine_m(p.anchor[0], p.anchor[1], c.lat, c.lon) / 1000
            if d_km > radius_km:
                continue
            need, part = pick
            fit = (1 - d_km / radius_km) ** 0.7
            ranked.append((need * (0.35 + 0.65 * fit) * (0.6 + 0.4 * p.score), need, fit, c, part, d_km))
        ranked.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)
        chosen: list[Candidate] = []
        for _, need, fit, c, part, d_km in ranked:
            if len(chosen) >= cap:
                break
            if (c.id, part) in committed:
                continue
            if any(haversine_m(c.lat, c.lon, o.lat, o.lon) < rs.ROAD_MIN_SPACING_M for o in chosen):
                continue
            chosen.append(c)
            created.append(_make(tenant_id, p, c, part, wins[part], d_km, need, fit))
            committed.add((c.id, part))
            pairs.add((uid, c.id))
            road_load[uid] += 1
            p.load += 1
    if created:
        db.add_all(created)
        await db.flush()
    return created


async def needs_near(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float, radius_km: float,
                     limit: int) -> list[dict]:
    """Vue admin : routes qui manquent le plus de mesures autour d'un point, avec la plage conseillée."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    wins = eligible_windows(now)
    out = []
    for c in await load_candidates(db, tenant_id, lat, lon, radius_km, now):
        sl = slots(c, wins)
        d = haversine_m(lat, lon, c.lat, c.lon)
        if not sl or d > radius_km * 1000:
            continue
        need, part = sl[0]
        out.append({"segment_id": str(c.id), "name": c.name or c.ref, "highway": c.highway, "rank": c.rank,
                    "lat": c.lat, "lon": c.lon, "distance_m": round(d), "need": round(need, 3),
                    "best_day_part": part, "window_start_at": wins[part][0], "window_end_at": wins[part][1],
                    "counts_by_day_part": c.counts})
    out.sort(key=lambda r: r["need"], reverse=True)
    return out[:limit]

# highway -> (rang, poids de trafic attendu)

}


class OverpassBusy(Exception):
    pass


class OverpassTooHeavy(Exception):
    pass


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ géométrie

def segment_uuid(way_id: int, seq: int) -> uuid.UUID:
    return uuid.uuid5(_NS, f"{way_id}:{seq}")


def _hav(a: tuple[float, float], b: tuple[float, float]) -> float:
    la1, lo1, la2, lo2 = (math.radians(v) for v in (a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1]))
    h = math.sin((la2 - la1) / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(la1) * math.cos(la2) * math.sin((lo2 - lo1) / 2) ** 2
    return 2 * 6371000.0 * math.asin(math.sqrt(h))


def split_way(pts: list[tuple[float, float]], target_m: float, min_m: float) -> list[tuple[int, int, float]]:
    """Découpe une route en tronçons coupés sur de vrais sommets (la connectivité est conservée)."""
    n = len(pts)
    if n < 2:
        return []
    steps = [_hav(pts[i], pts[i + 1]) for i in range(n - 1)]
    cuts, acc = [0], 0.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        acc += steps[i - 1]
        if acc >= target_m and i < n - 1:
            cuts.append(i)
            acc = 0.0
    cuts.append(n - 1)
    if len(cuts) > 2 and sum(steps[cuts[-2]:cuts[-1]]) < min_m:
        cuts.pop(-2)                                        # le petit reste est fusionné au tronçon précédent
    return [(a, b, sum(steps[a:b])) for a, b in zip(cuts, cuts[1:])]


def _point_along(pts: list[tuple[float, float]], frac: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    steps = [_hav(pts[i], pts[i + 1]) for i in range(len(pts) - 1)]
    target, acc = sum(steps) * frac, 0.0
    for k, s in enumerate(steps):
        if s > 0 and acc + s >= target:
            t = (target - acc) / s
            return (pts[k][0] + (pts[k + 1][0] - pts[k][0]) * t, pts[k][1] + (pts[k + 1][1] - pts[k][1]) * t)
        acc += s
    return pts[-1]


def rows_from_elements(elements: list[dict], now: datetime) -> tuple[list[dict], list[int]]:
    allowed = set(rs.highways)
    rows: list[dict] = []
    way_ids: list[int] = []
    for el in elements:
        if el.get("type") != "way":
            continue
        tags = el.get("tags") or {}
        hw = tags.get("highway")
        if hw not in allowed or hw not in HIGHWAYS or tags.get("area") == "yes":
            continue
        geom, nodes = el.get("geometry") or [], el.get("nodes") or []
        if len(geom) != len(nodes):
            continue
        pts, nds = [], []
        for g, nid in zip(geom, nodes):
            if g:
                pts.append((g["lat"], g["lon"]))
                nds.append(int(nid))
        if len(pts) < 2:
            continue
        rank, weight = HIGHWAYS[hw]
        way_id = int(el["id"])
        way_ids.append(way_id)
        name = (tags.get("name") or "").strip()[:160] or None
        ref = (tags.get("ref") or "").strip()[:60] or None
        oneway = tags.get("oneway") in ("yes", "true", "1") or tags.get("junction") == "roundabout"
        maxspeed = (tags.get("maxspeed") or "")[:20] or None
        try:
            lanes = int(str(tags.get("lanes", "")).split(";")[0])
        except ValueError:
            lanes = None
        lanes = lanes if lanes and 0 < lanes <= 20 else None
        for seq, (a, b, length) in enumerate(split_way(pts, rs.ROAD_SEGMENT_TARGET_M, rs.ROAD_SEGMENT_MIN_M)):
            sub = pts[a:b + 1]
            if length < 1.0:
                continue
            mlat, mlon = _point_along(sub, 0.5)
            rows.append({
                "id": segment_uuid(way_id, seq), "osm_way_id": way_id, "seq": seq,
                "start_node": nds[a], "end_node": nds[b], "highway": hw, "road_rank": rank,
                "class_weight": weight, "name": name, "ref": ref, "oneway": oneway, "maxspeed": maxspeed,
                "lanes": lanes, "length_m": round(length, 1), "mid_lat": mlat, "mid_lon": mlon,
                "h3_index": h3.latlng_to_cell(mlat, mlon, settings.H3_RESOLUTION),
                "wkt": "LINESTRING(" + ",".join(f"{lo:.6f} {la:.6f}" for la, lo in sub) + ")",
                "imported_at": now})
    return rows, way_ids


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Overpass

def build_query(bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> str:
    w, s, e, n = bbox
    hw = "|".join(rs.highways)
    area = flt = ""
    if rs.ROAD_COUNTRY_ISO:
        area = f'area["ISO3166-1"="{rs.ROAD_COUNTRY_ISO}"][admin_level=2]->.a;'
        flt = "(area.a)"
    return (f"[out:json][timeout:{rs.ROAD_OVERPASS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}];{area}"
            f'way["highway"~"^({hw})$"]({s},{w},{n},{e}){flt};out body geom;')


async def fetch_overpass(bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> dict:
    query = build_query(bbox)
    last: Exception = OverpassBusy("no_endpoint")
    headers = {"User-Agent": "SunuSound/1.0 (road network import)"}
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=rs.ROAD_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, headers=headers) as client:
        for url in rs.urls:
            try:
                r = await client.post(url, data={"data": query})
            except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
                last = OverpassBusy(type(exc).__name__)
                continue
            if r.status_code == 200:
                try:
                    data = r.json()
                except ValueError:
                    last = OverpassBusy("bad_json")
                    continue
                remark = str(data.get("remark") or "").lower()
                if "runtime error" in remark or "out of memory" in remark or "timed out" in remark:
                    raise OverpassTooHeavy(remark[:120])
                return data
            last = OverpassBusy(f"http_{r.status_code}")
    raise last


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tuiles d'import

def tile_key(b: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> str:
    return f"{b[0]:.5f}_{b[1]:.5f}_{b[2]:.5f}_{b[3]:.5f}"


async def plan_tiles(db: AsyncSession) -> int:
    w0, s0, e0, n0 = rs.bbox
    step = rs.ROAD_TILE_SIZE_DEG
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = []
    x = w0
    while x < e0 - 1e-9:
        y = s0
        while y < n0 - 1e-9:
            b = (round(x, 5), round(y, 5), round(min(x + step, e0), 5), round(min(y + step, n0), 5))
            rows.append({"key": tile_key(b), "west": b[0], "south": b[1], "east": b[2], "north": b[3],
                         "size_deg": step, "status": "pending", "attempts": 0, "updated_at": now})
            y += step
        x += step
    for i in range(0, len(rows), 500):
        await db.execute(pg_insert(RoadImportTile).values(rows[i:i + 500]).on_conflict_do_nothing(
            index_elements=["key"]))
    await db.commit()
    return len(rows)


async def network_ready(db: AsyncSession) -> bool:
    total, open_ = (await db.execute(
        select(func.count(), func.count().filter(RoadImportTile.status.in_(("pending", "queued", "running", "failed"))))
        .select_from(RoadImportTile))).one()
    return int(total) > 0 and int(open_) == 0


async def version(redis: Redis) -> int:
    return int(await redis.get("roads:version") or 0)


async def ensure_network(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, force: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Planifie l'import au premier lancement, puis relance les tuiles en attente, échouées ou bloquées."""
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(RoadImportTile))).scalar_one()
    if total == 0:
        if not rs.ROAD_AUTO_IMPORT and not force:
            return {"status": "disabled", "queued": []}
        planned = await plan_tiles(db)
        log.info("roads_import_planned", extra={"task": f"tiles={planned}"})
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    stale = now - timedelta(minutes=rs.ROAD_STALE_MINUTES)
    retry_failed = now - timedelta(hours=6)
    tiles = (await db.execute(select(RoadImportTile).where(RoadImportTile.status.in_(
        ("pending", "failed", "queued", "running"))).order_by(RoadImportTile.key))).scalars().all()
    keys: list[str] = []
    for t in tiles:
        if t.status == "failed" and not force and t.updated_at > retry_failed:
            continue
        if t.status in ("queued", "running") and t.updated_at > stale and not force:
            continue
        if t.status == "failed":
            t.attempts = 0
        t.status, t.updated_at = "queued", now
        keys.append(t.key)
    await db.commit()
    return {"status": "ok", "queued": keys, "ready": await network_ready(db)}


async def _split(db: AsyncSession, tile: RoadImportTile) -> list[str]:
    w, s, e, n = tile.west, tile.south, tile.east, tile.north
    mw, mn = round((w + e) / 2, 5), round((s + n) / 2, 5)
    boxes = [(w, s, mw, mn), (mw, s, e, mn), (w, mn, mw, n), (mw, mn, e, n)]
    size = max(e - w, n - s) / 2
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    keys = []
    for b in boxes:
        k = tile_key(b)
        keys.append(k)
        await db.execute(pg_insert(RoadImportTile).values(
            key=k, west=b[0], south=b[1], east=b[2], north=b[3], size_deg=size, status="queued",
            attempts=0, updated_at=now).on_conflict_do_nothing(index_elements=["key"]))
    tile.status, tile.error, tile.finished_at, tile.updated_at = "split", None, now, now
    await db.commit()
    return keys


_UPSERT = text("""
INSERT INTO road_segments (id, osm_way_id, seq, start_node, end_node, highway, road_rank, class_weight, name, ref,
    oneway, maxspeed, lanes, length_m, mid_lat, mid_lon, h3_index, geom, active, imported_at)
VALUES (:id, :osm_way_id, :seq, :start_node, :end_node, :highway, :road_rank, :class_weight, :name, :ref,
    :oneway, :maxspeed, :lanes, :length_m, :mid_lat, :mid_lon, :h3_index,
    ST_SetSRID(ST_GeomFromText(:wkt), 4326), true, :imported_at)
ON CONFLICT (osm_way_id, seq) DO UPDATE SET
    start_node = EXCLUDED.start_node, end_node = EXCLUDED.end_node, highway = EXCLUDED.highway,
    road_rank = EXCLUDED.road_rank, class_weight = EXCLUDED.class_weight, name = EXCLUDED.name,
    ref = EXCLUDED.ref, oneway = EXCLUDED.oneway, maxspeed = EXCLUDED.maxspeed, lanes = EXCLUDED.lanes,
    length_m = EXCLUDED.length_m, mid_lat = EXCLUDED.mid_lat, mid_lon = EXCLUDED.mid_lon,
    h3_index = EXCLUDED.h3_index, geom = EXCLUDED.geom, active = true, imported_at = EXCLUDED.imported_at
""")
_DEACTIVATE = text("UPDATE road_segments SET active = false "
                   "WHERE osm_way_id = ANY(:ids) AND imported_at < :since AND active")


async def run_tile(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, key: str) -> dict:
    """Importe une tuile. Retourne {children}, {retry_in}, {failed}, {done} ou {skipped}."""
    tile = await db.get(RoadImportTile, key)
    if tile is None or tile.status in ("done", "split"):
        return {"skipped": True}
    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    tile.status, tile.attempts, tile.updated_at = "running", tile.attempts + 1, started
    await db.commit()
    bbox = (tile.west, tile.south, tile.east, tile.north)
    splittable = max(tile.east - tile.west, tile.north - tile.south) > rs.ROAD_TILE_MIN_DEG * 1.01
    try:
        data = await fetch_overpass(bbox)
        elements = data.get("elements") or []
        if len(elements) > rs.ROAD_TILE_MAX_WAYS and splittable:
            return {"children": await _split(db, tile), "split": True}
        rows, way_ids = rows_from_elements(elements, started)
        for i in range(0, len(rows), rs.ROAD_INSERT_CHUNK):
            await db.execute(_UPSERT, rows[i:i + rs.ROAD_INSERT_CHUNK])
        for i in range(0, len(way_ids), 20000):
            await db.execute(_DEACTIVATE, {"ids": way_ids[i:i + 20000], "since": started})
        tile.status, tile.ways, tile.segments, tile.error = "done", len(way_ids), len(rows), None
        tile.finished_at = tile.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        await db.commit()
    except (OverpassTooHeavy, OverpassBusy) as exc:
        await db.rollback()
        tile = await db.get(RoadImportTile, key)
        if (isinstance(exc, OverpassTooHeavy) or tile.attempts >= 2) and splittable:
            return {"children": await _split(db, tile), "split": True}
        if tile.attempts >= rs.ROAD_TILE_MAX_ATTEMPTS:
            tile.status, tile.error = "failed", str(exc)[:200]
            tile.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            await db.commit()
            return {"failed": True}
        tile.status, tile.error, tile.updated_at = "queued", str(exc)[:200], datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        await db.commit()
        return {"retry_in": min(900, 60 * tile.attempts)}
    except Exception as exc:
        await db.rollback()
        tile = await db.get(RoadImportTile, key)
        tile.status, tile.error = "failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:200]
        tile.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        await db.commit()
        log.exception("road_tile_failed", extra={"task": key})
        return {"failed": True}
    if await network_ready(db):
        await redis.incr("roads:version")                 # invalide les caches du réseau
        log.info("roads_network_ready")
    return {"done": True, "ways": len(way_ids), "segments": len(rows)}


async def import_status(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis) -> dict:
    by = dict((await db.execute(select(RoadImportTile.status, func.count())
                                .group_by(RoadImportTile.status))).all())
    segs, km = (await db.execute(select(func.count(), func.coalesce(func.sum(RoadSegment.length_m), 0))
                                 .where(RoadSegment.active.is_(True)))).one()
    return {"tiles": by, "ready": await network_ready(db), "segments": int(segs),
            "km": round(float(km) / 1000, 1), "version": await version(redis)}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ rattachement mesure -> tronçon

_MATCH = text("""
WITH todo AS (
    SELECT id, geom FROM measurements
    WHERE tenant_id = :tid AND road_matched_at IS NULL AND location_anonymized = false
    ORDER BY measured_at DESC LIMIT :lim FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
), best AS (
    SELECT t.id AS mid, r.id AS sid, r.dist AS dist
    FROM todo t LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT s.id, ST_Distance(s.geom::geography, t.geom::geography) AS dist
        FROM road_segments s
        WHERE s.active AND ST_DWithin(s.geom, t.geom, :deg)
        ORDER BY s.geom <-> t.geom LIMIT 1
    ) r ON true
)
UPDATE measurements m SET
    road_segment_id = CASE WHEN best.dist <= :max_m THEN best.sid END,
    road_distance_m = CASE WHEN best.dist <= :max_m THEN best.dist END,
    road_matched_at = CASE WHEN best.dist <= :max_m OR :final THEN now() END
FROM best WHERE m.id = best.mid
""")


async def match_measurements(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    """Rattache les mesures non traitées au tronçon le plus proche. Tant que l'import n'est pas terminé,
    une mesure sans tronçon reste à retraiter (elle peut tomber dans une zone pas encore importée)."""
    if not (await db.execute(select(RoadSegment.id).limit(1))).first():
        return 0
    ready = await network_ready(db)
    deg = rs.ROAD_MATCH_MAX_M / 111320.0 * 1.6
    res = await db.execute(_MATCH, {"tid": tenant_id, "lim": rs.ROAD_MATCH_BATCH, "deg": deg,
                                    "max_m": rs.ROAD_MATCH_MAX_M, "final": ready})
    return res.rowcount or 0


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ carte : réseau et état de bruit

def rank_for(zoom: float | None, max_rank: int | None) -> int:
    if max_rank:
        return max_rank
    if zoom is None:
        return 3
    for z, r in ((8, 2), (11, 3), (13, 4), (15, 5)):
        if zoom < z:
            return r
    return 6


def check_bbox(bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> None:
    if bbox[2] - bbox[0] > rs.ROAD_MAP_MAX_BBOX_DEG or bbox[3] - bbox[1] > rs.ROAD_MAP_MAX_BBOX_DEG:
        raise HTTPException(422, "bbox_too_large")


def _snap(bbox, step: float):
    return (round(math.floor(bbox[0] / step) * step, 5), round(math.floor(bbox[1] / step) * step, 5),
            round(math.ceil(bbox[2] / step) * step, 5), round(math.ceil(bbox[3] / step) * step, 5))


def intensity(db: float) -> float:
    return round(clamp01((db - settings.NOISE_REF_MIN_DB) / (settings.NOISE_REF_MAX_DB - settings.NOISE_REF_MIN_DB)), 3)


def baseline_state() -> dict:
    return {"has_data": False, "laeq_db": rs.ROAD_BASELINE_DB, "lmax_db": None, "measurement_count": 0,
            "device_count": 0, "confidence": 0.0, "last_measurement_at": None,
            "intensity": intensity(rs.ROAD_BASELINE_DB)}


def _state(r, now: datetime) -> dict:
    laeq = round(float(r.laeq), 1)
    return {"has_data": True, "laeq_db": laeq, "lmax_db": round(float(r.lmax), 1), "measurement_count": r.n,
            "device_count": r.devices,
            "confidence": confidence_of(r.n, r.devices, int(r.calibrated or 0), r.last, now),
            "last_measurement_at": r.last.isoformat() if r.last else None, "intensity": intensity(laeq)}


async def network_features(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, bbox, max_rank: int) -> dict:
    """Géométrie statique du réseau (mise en cache : identique pour tous, change seulement après un import)."""
    check_bbox(bbox)
    b = _snap(bbox, 0.02)
    key = f"roads:net:{await version(redis)}:{max_rank}:{b[0]}:{b[1]}:{b[2]}:{b[3]}"
    etag = '"' + hashlib.sha1(key.encode()).hexdigest()[:20] + '"'
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        d = json.loads(hit)
        return {**d, "etag": etag}
    cap = rs.ROAD_MAP_MAX_FEATURES
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(RoadSegment.id, RoadSegment.highway, RoadSegment.name, RoadSegment.ref, RoadSegment.road_rank,
               RoadSegment.length_m, RoadSegment.oneway, RoadSegment.maxspeed,
               func.ST_AsGeoJSON(RoadSegment.geom, 6))
        .where(RoadSegment.active.is_(True), RoadSegment.road_rank <= max_rank,
               func.ST_Intersects(RoadSegment.geom, func.ST_MakeEnvelope(b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3], 4326)))
        .order_by(RoadSegment.road_rank, RoadSegment.length_m.desc()).limit(cap + 1))).all()
    truncated = len(rows) > cap
    feats = [{"type": "Feature", "id": str(sid), "geometry": json.loads(gj),
              "properties": {"id": str(sid), "highway": hw, "name": name, "ref": ref, "rank": rank,
                             "length_m": ln, "oneway": ow, "maxspeed": ms}}
             for sid, hw, name, ref, rank, ln, ow, ms, gj in rows[:cap]]
    payload = {"features": feats, "truncated": truncated}
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(payload), ex=rs.ROAD_NETWORK_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)
    return {**payload, "etag": etag}


async def road_states(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter, include_indicative: bool, bbox,
                      max_rank: int, min_devices: int) -> dict[str, dict]:
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    cols = _agg_cols()
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    stmt = (select(Measurement.road_segment_id.label("sid"), *[c.label(k) for k, c in cols.items()])
            .join(RoadSegment, RoadSegment.id == Measurement.road_segment_id)
            .where(*_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, tf.day_part, statuses(include_indicative)),
                   RoadSegment.active.is_(True), RoadSegment.road_rank <= max_rank,
                   func.ST_Intersects(RoadSegment.geom,
                                      func.ST_MakeEnvelope(bbox[0], bbox[1], bbox[2], bbox[3], 4326)))
            .group_by(Measurement.road_segment_id).limit(rs.ROAD_MAP_MAX_FEATURES * 2))
    if min_devices > 1:                                    # k-anonymat : même règle que la carte
        stmt = stmt.having(cols["devices"] >= min_devices)
    return {str(r.sid): _state(r, now) for r in (await db.execute(stmt)).all()}


async def cached_states(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter,
                        include_indicative: bool, bbox, max_rank: int, min_devices: int) -> dict[str, dict]:
    check_bbox(bbox)
    b = _snap(bbox, 0.02)
    raw = json.dumps([str(tenant_id), tf.period.value, str(tf.date_from), str(tf.date_to), tf.day_part,
                      include_indicative, b, max_rank, min_devices], default=str)
    key = "roads:st:" + hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)
    data = await road_states(db, tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, b, max_rank, min_devices)
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(data), ex=rs.ROAD_STATE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)
    return data


def merge(net: dict, states: dict[str, dict]) -> dict:
    feats = []
    for f in net["features"]:
        st = states.get(f["properties"]["id"]) or baseline_state()
        feats.append({**f, "properties": {**f["properties"], **st}})
    return {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": feats, "truncated": net["truncated"],
            "baseline_db": rs.ROAD_BASELINE_DB}


async def segment_detail(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, segment_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter,
                         include_indicative: bool, min_devices: int) -> dict:
    row = (await db.execute(select(RoadSegment, func.ST_AsGeoJSON(RoadSegment.geom, 6))
                            .where(RoadSegment.id == segment_id))).first()
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "road_segment_not_found")
    seg, gj = row
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    stats = statuses(include_indicative)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    cols = _agg_cols()
    r = (await db.execute(select(*[c.label(k) for k, c in cols.items()]).where(
        *_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, tf.day_part, stats), Measurement.road_segment_id == segment_id))).one()
    state = _state(r, now) if r.n and r.devices >= min_devices else baseline_state()
    pcols = _agg_cols()
    pstmt = (select(Measurement.day_part, *[c.label(k) for k, c in pcols.items()])
             .where(*_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, "all", stats), Measurement.road_segment_id == segment_id)
             .group_by(Measurement.day_part))
    if min_devices > 1:
        pstmt = pstmt.having(pcols["devices"] >= min_devices)
    parts = [{"day_part": p.day_part.value, "laeq_db": round(float(p.laeq), 1), "measurement_count": p.n}
             for p in (await db.execute(pstmt)).all()]
    nodes = [seg.start_node, seg.end_node]
    conn = (await db.execute(select(RoadSegment.id, RoadSegment.name, RoadSegment.highway).where(
        RoadSegment.active.is_(True), RoadSegment.id != seg.id,
        or_(RoadSegment.start_node.in_(nodes), RoadSegment.end_node.in_(nodes))).limit(20))).all()
    return {"id": str(seg.id), "osm_way_id": seg.osm_way_id, "highway": seg.highway, "rank": seg.road_rank,
            "name": seg.name, "ref": seg.ref, "oneway": seg.oneway, "maxspeed": seg.maxspeed, "lanes": seg.lanes,
            "length_m": seg.length_m, "target": {"lat": seg.mid_lat, "lon": seg.mid_lon,
                                                 "radius_m": rs.ROAD_TARGET_RADIUS_M},
            "geometry": json.loads(gj), "state": state, "by_day_part": parts,
            "connected": [{"id": str(i), "name": n, "highway": h} for i, n, h in conn]}


async def stats(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    by_rank = (await db.execute(select(RoadSegment.road_rank, func.count(), func.sum(RoadSegment.length_m))
                                .where(RoadSegment.active.is_(True)).group_by(RoadSegment.road_rank)
                                .order_by(RoadSegment.road_rank))).all()
    total = sum(int(n) for _, n, _ in by_rank)
    covered = (await db.execute(select(func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.road_segment_id))).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.road_segment_id.is_not(None),
        Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=rs.ROAD_COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS),
        Measurement.quality_status.in_(_USABLE)))).scalar_one()
    n_all, n_matched = (await db.execute(select(
        func.count(), func.count().filter(Measurement.road_segment_id.is_not(None))).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=30)))).one()
    return {"segments": total,
            "by_rank": [{"rank": r, "segments": int(n), "km": round(float(km or 0) / 1000, 1)} for r, n, km in by_rank],
            "covered_segments": int(covered),
            "coverage_share": round(int(covered) / total, 4) if total else 0.0,
            "measurements_30d": int(n_all), "matched_to_road_30d": int(n_matched),
            "matched_share": round(int(n_matched) / int(n_all), 3) if n_all else 0.0}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/statistics.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import Float, case, distinct, func, literal_column, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.collection import CollectionProposal
from app.models.devices import DeviceModel
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, DayPart, QualityStatus
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell, NoiseAggregation
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter, OverviewOut, TimeseriesPoint, UnderDocumentedCell
from app.services.geo import period_dates, resolve_period

_TEN = literal_column("10.0")


async def overview(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter) -> OverviewOut:
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    base = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at >= start, Measurement.measured_at < end]
    usable = [*base, Measurement.quality_status.in_([QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative])]

    by_status = dict((await db.execute(select(Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(*base)
                                       .group_by(Measurement.quality_status))).all())
    by_part = dict((await db.execute(select(Measurement.day_part, func.count()).where(*usable)
                                     .group_by(Measurement.day_part))).all())
    active = (await db.execute(select(func.count(distinct(Measurement.contributor_hash))).where(*usable))).scalar_one()
    covered = (await db.execute(select(func.count(distinct(Measurement.cell_id))).where(
        *base, Measurement.quality_status == QualityStatus.validated))).scalar_one()
    total_usable, calibrated = (await db.execute(select(
        func.count(), func.sum(case((Measurement.calibration_status == CalibrationStatus.calibrated, 1), else_=0)))
        .where(*usable))).one()
    models = dict((await db.execute(select(DeviceModel.calibration_status, func.count()).where(
        DeviceModel.tenant_id == tenant_id).group_by(DeviceModel.calibration_status))).all())
    proposals = dict((await db.execute(select(CollectionProposal.status, func.count()).where(
        CollectionProposal.tenant_id == tenant_id, CollectionProposal.created_at >= start,
        CollectionProposal.created_at < end).group_by(CollectionProposal.status))).all())

    counts = (select(Measurement.cell_id, func.count().label("n")).where(
        *base, Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected).group_by(Measurement.cell_id).subquery())
    n = func.coalesce(counts.c.n, 0)
    weak = (await db.execute(
        select(GeographicCell.h3_index, n).outerjoin(counts, counts.c.cell_id == GeographicCell.id)
        .where(GeographicCell.tenant_id == tenant_id, n < settings.TARGET_MEASUREMENTS_PER_CELL)
        .order_by(n, GeographicCell.h3_index).limit(50))).all()

    return OverviewOut(
        period_start=start, period_end=end,
        measurements_by_status={k.value: v for k, v in by_status.items()},
        measurements_by_day_part={k.value: v for k, v in by_part.items()},
        active_contributors=active, cells_covered=covered,
        calibrated_share=round(calibrated / total_usable, 3) if total_usable else None,
        device_models_by_calibration={k.value: v for k, v in models.items()},
        proposals_by_status={k.value: v for k, v in proposals.items()},
        under_documented_cells=[UnderDocumentedCell(h3_index=h, measurement_count=c) for h, c in weak])


async def timeseries(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter,
                     include_indicative: bool) -> list[TimeseriesPoint]:
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    d_from, d_to = period_dates(start, end)
    stats = [QualityStatus.validated] + ([QualityStatus.indicative] if include_indicative else [])
    conds = [NoiseAggregation.tenant_id == tenant_id, NoiseAggregation.bucket_date >= d_from,
             NoiseAggregation.bucket_date <= d_to, NoiseAggregation.quality_status.in_(stats)]
    if tf.day_part != "all":
        conds.append(NoiseAggregation.day_part == DayPart(tf.day_part))
    en, du = func.sum(NoiseAggregation.energy_sum), func.sum(NoiseAggregation.duration_total)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date, func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count),
               _TEN * func.log(en / du, type_=Float), func.max(NoiseAggregation.lmax_db))
        .where(*conds).group_by(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date).order_by(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date))).all()
    return [TimeseriesPoint(date=d, measurement_count=int(n), laeq_db=round(float(l), 1) if l is not None else None,
                            lmax_db=round(float(mx), 1) if mx is not None else None) for d, n, l, mx in rows]


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/tenant.py
################################################################################

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.security import hash_email, hash_password, make_pseudonym
from app.db.session import bind_tenant
from app.models.collection import TenantAlgorithmState
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User
from app.schemas.schemas import TenantCreate, TenantUpdate
from app.services.collection import base_weights
from app.services.audit import audit
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import uuid


async def create_tenant(db: AsyncSession, payload: TenantCreate) -> Tenant:
    tenant = Tenant(id=uuid.uuid4(), slug=payload.slug, name=payload.name,
                    allow_registration=payload.allow_registration)
    db.add(tenant)
    try:
        await db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(409, "slug_already_used")
    await bind_tenant(db, tenant.id)
    admin_id = uuid.uuid4()
    db.add(User(id=admin_id, tenant_id=tenant.id, email_hash=hash_email(tenant.id, payload.admin_email),
                password_hash=hash_password(payload.admin_password), role=Role.tenant_admin,
                pseudonym=make_pseudonym(tenant.id, admin_id)))
    db.add(TenantAlgorithmState(tenant_id=tenant.id, weights=base_weights(), samples_used=0,
                                updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
    await audit(db, None, "tenant.create", "tenant", tenant.id, tenant_id=tenant.id)
    await db.commit()
    return tenant


async def list_tenants(db: AsyncSession) -> list[Tenant]:
    return list((await db.execute(select(Tenant).order_by(Tenant.created_at))).scalars())


async def update_tenant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, payload: TenantUpdate) -> Tenant:
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, tenant_id)
    if tenant is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "tenant_not_found")
    for field, value in payload.model_dump(exclude_none=True).items():
        setattr(tenant, field, value)
    await db.commit()
    return tenant
